   54 | function hasBrowserWindow(): boolean {
   55 |   return typeof globalThis.window !== "undefined";
   56 | }
   57 | 
   58 | function getBrowserWindow(): Window | null {
   59 |   return hasBrowserWindow() ? globalThis.window : null;
   60 | }
   61 | 
   62 | function prefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
   63 |   const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   64 |   if (!browserWindow) return true;
   65 | 
   66 |   return (
   67 |     browserWindow.matchMedia?.("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)")?.matches ??
   68 |     false
   69 |   );
   70 | }
   71 | 
   72 | function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
   73 |   const get = () => getBrowserWindow()?.matchMedia(query).matches ?? false;
   74 | 
   75 |   const [matches, setMatches] = React.useState<boolean>(get);
   76 | 
   77 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   78 |     const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   79 |     if (!browserWindow) return;
   80 | 
   81 |     const mediaQueryList = browserWindow.matchMedia(query);
   82 |     const onChange = () => setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   83 | 
   84 |     setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   85 |     mediaQueryList.addEventListener("change", onChange);
   86 | 
   87 |     return () => mediaQueryList.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
   88 |   }, [query]);
   89 | 
   90 |   return matches;
   91 | }

  535 |   }

  537 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  538 |     function targetNodeFromEvent(e: MouseEvent): Node | null {
  539 |       return e.target instanceof Node ? e.target : null;
  540 |     }
  541 | 
  542 |     function closeComposerIfEmpty(target: Node) {
  543 |       const composerEl = composerWrapRef.current;
  544 |       const clickedInComposer = Boolean(composerEl?.contains(target));
  545 |       const hasDraft = Boolean(draftRef.current.trim());
  546 | 
  547 |       if (!clickedInComposer && !hasDraft) {
  548 |         setComposerStage("collapsed");
  549 |       }
  550 |     }
  551 | 
  552 |     function closeEmptyReplies(target: Node) {
  553 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  554 |         replyDraftsRef.current,
  555 |       )) {
  556 |         const wrapper = replyWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  557 |         const shouldClose =
  558 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  559 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  560 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  561 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  562 | 
  563 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReplyDraft(commentId);
  564 |       }
  565 |     }
  566 | 
  567 |     function closeEmptyEdits(target: Node) {
  568 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  569 |         editDraftsRef.current,
  570 |       )) {
  571 |         const wrapper = editWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  572 |         const shouldClose =
  573 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  574 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  575 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  576 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  577 | 
  578 |         if (shouldClose) deleteEditDraft(commentId);
  579 |       }
  580 |     }
  581 | 
  582 |     function closeAutoClosableReports(target: Node) {
  583 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  584 |         reportDraftsRef.current,
  585 |       )) {
  586 |         const wrapper = reportWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  587 |         const canAutoClose =
  588 |           Boolean(draftState.done) || !draftState.reason.trim();
  589 |         const shouldClose =
  590 |           draftState.open &&
  591 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  592 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  593 |           canAutoClose;
  594 | 
  595 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReportDraft(commentId);
  596 |       }
  597 |     }
  598 | 
  599 |     function onMouseDown(e: MouseEvent) {
  600 |       const target = targetNodeFromEvent(e);
  601 |       if (!target) return;
  602 | 
  603 |       closeComposerIfEmpty(target);
  604 |       closeEmptyReplies(target);
  605 |       closeEmptyEdits(target);
  606 |       closeAutoClosableReports(target);
  607 |     }
  608 | 
  609 |     globalThis.document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  610 |     return () =>
  611 |       globalThis.document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  612 |   }, []);
  613 |

  615 |     const h = parseHash();
  616 |     const rid = (h.rootId ?? "").trim();
  617 |     setFocusedRootId(rid);
  618 |   }, [threadKey]);
  619 | 
  620 |   const rootsForView = React.useMemo(() => {
  621 |     const roots = rootsForRender ?? [];

  623 |     return roots.filter((r) => r.rootId === focusedRootId);
  624 |   }, [rootsForRender, focusedRootId]);

  626 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  627 |     if (!flipPendingRef.current) return;
  628 |     flipPendingRef.current = false;
  629 | 
  630 |     if (prefersReducedMotion()) return;
  631 | 
  632 |     const prevRects = flipRectsRef.current;
  633 |     for (const [id, el] of Object.entries(rootElByIdRef.current)) {
  634 |       if (!el) continue;
  635 |       const prev = prevRects[id];
  636 |       if (!prev) continue;
  637 |       const next = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  638 |       const dy = prev.top - next.top;
  639 |       if (!dy) continue;
  640 | 
  641 |       el.style.transform = `translateY(${dy}px)`;
  642 |       el.style.transition = "transform 0s";
  643 | 
  644 |       globalThis.window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  645 |         el.style.transition = "transform 220ms ease-out";
  646 |         el.style.transform = "translateY(0)";
  647 |       });
  648 | 
  649 |       const cleanup = () => {
  650 |         el.style.transition = "";
  651 |         el.style.transform = "";
  652 |         el.removeEventListener("transitionend", cleanup);
  653 |       };
  654 |       el.addEventListener("transitionend", cleanup);
  655 |     }
  656 |   }, [rootsForRender]);

  707 |   function measurePanelY() {

   22 | function makeLinkSafe(href: string): string | null {
   23 |   const h = (href ?? "").trim();
   24 |   if (!h) return null;
   25 | 
   26 |   if (h.startsWith("#") || h.startsWith("/")) return h;
   27 | 
   28 |   try {
   29 |     const withProtocol = /^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z\d+\-.]*:/.test(h)
   30 |       ? h
   31 |       : `https://${h}`;
   32 |     const u = new URL(withProtocol);
   33 |     const p = (u.protocol || "").toLowerCase();
   34 |     if (p === "http:" || p === "https:" || p === "mailto:") return u.toString();
   35 |     return null;
   36 |   } catch {
   37 |     return null;
   38 |   }
   39 | }
   40 | 
   41 | function isJsonDoc(v: unknown): v is JSONContent {
   42 |   return (
   43 |     !!v && typeof v === "object" && (v as { type?: unknown }).type === "doc"
   44 |   );
   45 | }

  244 | function applyListToggle(editor: Editor, mode: "bullet" | "ordered") {
  245 |   const chain = editor.chain().focus();
  246 | 
  247 |   if (mode === "bullet") {
  248 |     if (editor.isActive("orderedList")) {
  249 |       chain.liftListItem("listItem").toggleBulletList().run();
  250 |       return;
  251 |     }
  252 |     chain.toggleBulletList().run();
  253 |     return;
  254 |   }
  255 | 
  256 |   if (editor.isActive("bulletList")) {
  257 |     chain.liftListItem("listItem").toggleOrderedList().run();
  258 |     return;
  259 |   }
  260 |   chain.toggleOrderedList().run();
  261 | }
  262 | 
  263 | function applyBlockquoteToggle(editor: Editor) {
  264 |   editor.chain().focus().toggleBlockquote().run();
  265 | }
  266 | 
  267 | function getSelectedText(editor: Editor): string {
  268 |   const { from, to, empty } = editor.state.selection;
  269 |   if (empty) return "";
  270 |   return editor.state.doc.textBetween(from, to, " ").trim();
  271 | }
  272 | 
  273 | function openLinkDraft(editor: Editor, setDraft: (next: LinkDraft) => void) {
  274 |   const selectedText = getSelectedText(editor);
  275 |   const currentHref = String(editor.getAttributes("link")?.href ?? "").trim();
  276 | 
  277 |   setDraft({
  278 |     open: true,
  279 |     href: currentHref,
  280 |     text: selectedText,
  281 |   });
  282 | }
  283 | 
  284 | function submitLinkDraft(editor: Editor, draft: LinkDraft, close: () => void) {
  285 |   const safeHref = makeLinkSafe(draft.href);
  286 |   if (!safeHref) {
  287 |     close();
  288 |     editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").unsetLink().run();
  289 |     return;
  290 |   }
  291 | 
  292 |   const selectedText = getSelectedText(editor);
  293 |   const explicitText = draft.text.trim();
  294 | 
  295 |   editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").unsetLink().run();
  296 | 
  297 |   if (selectedText) {
  298 |     editor.chain().focus().setLink({ href: safeHref }).run();
  299 |     close();
  300 |     return;
  301 |   }
  302 | 
  303 |   const textToInsert = explicitText || safeHref;
  304 |   editor
  305 |     .chain()
  306 |     .focus()
  307 |     .insertContent(textToInsert)
  308 |     .setTextSelection({
  309 |       from: editor.state.selection.from - textToInsert.length,
  310 |       to: editor.state.selection.from,
  311 |     })
  312 |     .setLink({ href: safeHref })
  313 |     .run();
  314 | 
  315 |   close();
  316 | }

  318 | function TipTapToolbar(props: { editor: Editor | null; disabled?: boolean }) {
  319 |   const editor = props.editor;
  320 |   const disabled = Boolean(props.disabled);
  321 | 
  322 |   const [linkDraft, setLinkDraft] = React.useState<LinkDraft>({
  323 |     open: false,
  324 |     href: "",
  325 |     text: "",
  326 |   });
  327 | 
  328 |   const hrefInputRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  329 | 
  330 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  331 |     if (!linkDraft.open) return;
  332 |     const t = window.setTimeout(() => hrefInputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
  333 |     return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  334 |   }, [linkDraft.open]);
  335 | 
  336 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  337 |     if (!editor) return;
  338 |     const handleSelectionUpdate = () => {
  339 |       if (!linkDraft.open) return;
  340 |       const selectedText = getSelectedText(editor);
  341 |       setLinkDraft((prev) => ({
  342 |         ...prev,
  343 |         text: selectedText || prev.text,
  344 |       }));
  345 |     };
  346 | 
  347 |     editor.on("selectionUpdate", handleSelectionUpdate);
  348 |     return () => {
  349 |       editor.off("selectionUpdate", handleSelectionUpdate);
  350 |     };
  351 |   }, [editor, linkDraft.open]);
  352 | 
  353 |   if (!editor) return null;
  354 | 
  355 |   return (
  356 |     <div className="px-3 py-2">
  357 |       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
  358 |         <ToolbarBtn
  359 |           title="Bold"
  360 |           disabled={disabled}
  361 |           active={editor.isActive("bold")}
  362 |           onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
  363 |         >
  364 |           <IconBold />
  365 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  366 | 
  367 |         <ToolbarBtn
  368 |           title="Italic"
  369 |           disabled={disabled}
  370 |           active={editor.isActive("italic")}
  371 |           onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
  372 |         >
  373 |           <IconItalic />
  374 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  375 | 
  376 |         <ToolbarBtn
  377 |           title="Strike"
  378 |           disabled={disabled}
  379 |           active={editor.isActive("strike")}
  380 |           onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
  381 |         >
  382 |           <IconStrike />
  383 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  384 | 
  385 |         <ToolbarBtn
  386 |           title="Inline code"
  387 |           disabled={disabled}
  388 |           active={editor.isActive("code")}
  389 |           onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleCode().run()}
  390 |         >
  391 |           <IconCode />
  392 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  393 | 
  394 |         <ToolbarSeparator />
  395 | 
  396 |         <ToolbarBtn
  397 |           title="Add/edit link"
  398 |           disabled={disabled}
  399 |           active={editor.isActive("link") || linkDraft.open}
  400 |           onClick={() => openLinkDraft(editor, setLinkDraft)}
  401 |         >
  402 |           <IconLink />
  403 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  404 | 
  405 |         <ToolbarBtn
  406 |           title="Bullet list"
  407 |           disabled={disabled}
  408 |           active={editor.isActive("bulletList")}
  409 |           onClick={() => applyListToggle(editor, "bullet")}
  410 |         >
  411 |           <IconBulletList />
  412 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  413 | 
  414 |         <ToolbarBtn
  415 |           title="Numbered list"
  416 |           disabled={disabled}
  417 |           active={editor.isActive("orderedList")}
  418 |           onClick={() => applyListToggle(editor, "ordered")}
  419 |         >
  420 |           <IconOrderedList />
  421 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  422 | 
  423 |         <ToolbarSeparator />
  424 | 
  425 |         <ToolbarBtn
  426 |           title="Blockquote"
  427 |           disabled={disabled}
  428 |           active={editor.isActive("blockquote")}
  429 |           onClick={() => applyBlockquoteToggle(editor)}
  430 |         >
  431 |           <IconQuote />
  432 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  433 | 
  434 |         <ToolbarBtn
  435 |           title="Remove formatting"
  436 |           disabled={disabled}
  437 |           onClick={() => {
  438 |             setLinkDraft({ open: false, href: "", text: "" });
  439 |             editor.chain().focus().unsetAllMarks().clearNodes().run();
  440 |           }}
  441 |         >
  442 |           <IconClear />
  443 |         </ToolbarBtn>
  444 |       </div>
  445 | 
  446 |       {linkDraft.open ? (
  447 |         <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.04] p-3">
  448 |           <div className="grid gap-2">
  449 |             <input
  450 |               ref={hrefInputRef}
  451 |               type="text"
  452 |               value={linkDraft.href}
  453 |               disabled={disabled}
  454 |               onChange={(e) =>
  455 |                 setLinkDraft((prev) => ({ ...prev, href: e.target.value }))
  456 |               }
  457 |               placeholder="Paste link URL"
  458 |               className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-black/[0.16] px-3 text-sm text-white/90 outline-none placeholder:text-white/30"
  459 |             />
  460 | 
  461 |             <input
  462 |               type="text"
  463 |               value={linkDraft.text}
  464 |               disabled={disabled}
  465 |               onChange={(e) =>
  466 |                 setLinkDraft((prev) => ({ ...prev, text: e.target.value }))
  467 |               }
  468 |               placeholder="Link text"
  469 |               className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-black/[0.16] px-3 text-sm text-white/90 outline-none placeholder:text-white/30"
  470 |             />
  471 |           </div>
  472 | 
  473 |           <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
  474 |             <button
  475 |               type="button"
  476 |               disabled={disabled}
  477 |               onClick={() => setLinkDraft({ open: false, href: "", text: "" })}
  478 |               className="inline-flex h-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-3 text-sm text-white/60 transition hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-white/92 disabled:opacity-35"
  479 |             >
  480 |               Cancel
  481 |             </button>
  482 | 
  483 |             <button
  484 |               type="button"
  485 |               disabled={disabled || !linkDraft.href.trim()}
  486 |               onClick={() =>
  487 |                 submitLinkDraft(editor, linkDraft, () =>
  488 |                   setLinkDraft({ open: false, href: "", text: "" }),
  489 |                 )
  490 |               }
  491 |               className="inline-flex h-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white/[0.08] px-3 text-sm text-white/92 transition hover:bg-white/[0.14] disabled:opacity-35"
  492 |             >
  493 |               Apply link
  494 |             </button>
  495 |           </div>
  496 |         </div>
  497 |       ) : null}
  498 |     </div>
  499 |   );
  500 | }
  501 | 
  502 | export default function TipTapEditor(props: {
  503 |   valuePlain: string;
  504 |   valueDoc?: unknown | null;
  505 |   disabled?: boolean;
  506 |   showToolbar?: boolean;
  507 |   placeholder?: string;
  508 |   autofocus?: boolean;
  509 |   onChangePlain: (plain: string) => void;
  510 |   onChangeDoc: (doc: TipTapDoc) => void;
  511 | }) {
  512 |   const {
  513 |     valuePlain,
  514 |     disabled,
  515 |     showToolbar = true,
  516 |     placeholder = "",
  517 |     autofocus = false,
  518 |     onChangePlain,
  519 |     onChangeDoc,
  520 |   } = props;
  521 | 
  522 |   const editor = useEditor({
  523 |     editable: !disabled,
  524 |     autofocus: autofocus ? "end" : false,
  525 |     immediatelyRender: false,
  526 |     extensions: [
  527 |       StarterKit.configure({
  528 |         heading: false,
  529 |         codeBlock: false,
  530 |         horizontalRule: false,
  531 |         link: false,
  532 |         blockquote: {
  533 |           HTMLAttributes: {
  534 |             class: "af-tiptap-blockquote",
  535 |           },
  536 |         },
  537 |         bulletList: {
  538 |           keepMarks: true,
  539 |           keepAttributes: false,
  540 |         },
  541 |         orderedList: {
  542 |           keepMarks: true,
  543 |           keepAttributes: false,
  544 |         },
  545 |       }),
  546 |       Link.configure({
  547 |         openOnClick: true,
  548 |         linkOnPaste: true,
  549 |         autolink: true,
  550 |         HTMLAttributes: {
  551 |           rel: "noopener noreferrer",
  552 |           target: "_blank",
  553 |           class: "af-tiptap-link",
  554 |         },
  555 |         validate: (href) => Boolean(makeLinkSafe(href)),
  556 |       }),
  557 |       Placeholder.configure({
  558 |         placeholder,
  559 |         emptyEditorClass:
  560 |           "before:pointer-events-none before:float-left before:h-0 before:text-white/35 before:content-[attr(data-placeholder)]",
  561 |       }),
  562 |     ],
  563 |     content: isJsonDoc(props.valueDoc)
  564 |       ? (props.valueDoc as JSONContent)
  565 |       : valuePlain
  566 |         ? valuePlain
  567 |         : "",
  568 |     editorProps: {
  569 |       attributes: {
  570 |         "data-placeholder": placeholder,
  571 |         class: [
  572 |           "tiptap-editor min-h-[120px] w-full px-4 py-3 text-[15px] leading-7 text-white/92 outline-none",
  573 |           "bg-transparent",
  574 |           "selection:bg-white/15",
  575 |           "disabled:opacity-50",
  576 |           "[&_p]:my-0",
  577 |           "[&_p+ul]:mt-3 [&_p+ol]:mt-3 [&_p+blockquote]:mt-3",
  578 |           "[&_ul]:my-3 [&_ul]:list-disc [&_ul]:pl-6",
  579 |           "[&_ol]:my-3 [&_ol]:list-decimal [&_ol]:pl-6",
  580 |           "[&_li]:my-1.5 [&_li>p]:my-0",
  581 |           "[&_blockquote]:my-3 [&_blockquote]:border-l-2 [&_blockquote]:border-white/20 [&_blockquote]:pl-4 [&_blockquote]:text-white/70 [&_blockquote]:italic",
  582 |           "[&_code]:rounded-md [&_code]:border [&_code]:border-white/10 [&_code]:bg-white/[0.06] [&_code]:px-1.5 [&_code]:py-0.5 [&_code]:font-mono [&_code]:text-[0.92em] [&_code]:text-white/95",
  583 |           "[&_a]:text-white [&_a]:underline [&_a]:decoration-white/35 [&_a]:underline-offset-4",
  584 |           "[&_a:hover]:decoration-white/70",
  585 |           "[&_.ProseMirror-focused]:outline-none",
  586 |         ].join(" "),
  587 |       },
  588 |       handleDOMEvents: {
  589 |         mousedown: (_view, event) => {
  590 |           const target = event.target;
  591 |           if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  592 |           if (target.closest("[data-af-link-toolbar]")) return true;
  593 |           return false;
  594 |         },
  595 |       },
  596 |     },
  597 |     onUpdate: ({ editor: nextEditor }) => {
  598 |       const json = nextEditor.getJSON() as TipTapDoc;
  599 |       lastAppliedExternalRef.current = JSON.stringify(json);
  600 |       const plain = (nextEditor.getText({ blockSeparator: "\n" }) ?? "").trim();
  601 |       onChangePlain(plain);
  602 |       onChangeDoc(json);
  603 |     },
  604 |   });
  605 | 
  606 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  607 |     if (!editor) return;
  608 |     editor.setEditable(!disabled);
  609 |   }, [editor, disabled]);
  610 | 
  611 |   const lastAppliedExternalRef = React.useRef<string>("");
  612 | 
  613 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  614 |     if (!editor) return;
  615 | 
  616 |     const nextDoc = props.valueDoc;
  617 |     const nextExternal = isJsonDoc(nextDoc)
  618 |       ? JSON.stringify(nextDoc)
  619 |       : JSON.stringify({ type: "plain", value: valuePlain ?? "" });
  620 | 
  621 |     const currentJson = JSON.stringify(editor.getJSON());
  622 | 
  623 |     if (nextExternal === lastAppliedExternalRef.current) return;
  624 |     if (isJsonDoc(nextDoc) && currentJson === JSON.stringify(nextDoc)) {
  625 |       lastAppliedExternalRef.current = nextExternal;
  626 |       return;
  627 |     }
  628 | 
  629 |     if (isJsonDoc(nextDoc)) {
  630 |       editor.commands.setContent(nextDoc as JSONContent, { emitUpdate: false });
  631 |       lastAppliedExternalRef.current = nextExternal;
  632 |       return;
  633 |     }
  634 | 
  635 |     const current = (editor.getText({ blockSeparator: "\n" }) ?? "").trim();
  636 |     const next = (valuePlain ?? "").trim();
  637 |     if (current === next) {
  638 |       lastAppliedExternalRef.current = nextExternal;
  639 |       return;
  640 |     }
  641 | 
  642 |     editor.commands.setContent(next, { emitUpdate: false });
  643 |     lastAppliedExternalRef.current = nextExternal;
  644 |   }, [editor, valuePlain, props.valueDoc]);
  645 | 
  646 |   return (
  647 |     <div className="bg-black/[0.16]">
  648 |       {showToolbar ? (
  649 |         <div data-af-link-toolbar="true">
  650 |           <TipTapToolbar editor={editor} disabled={disabled} />
  651 |         </div>
  652 |       ) : null}
  653 |       <EditorContent editor={editor} />
  654 |     </div>
  655 |   );
  656 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/(site)/exegesis/[displayId]/TipTapReadOnly.tsx =====
    1 | // web/app/(site)/exegesis/[recordingId]/TipTapReadOnly.tsx
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import { EditorContent, useEditor } from "@tiptap/react";
    6 | import StarterKit from "@tiptap/starter-kit";
    7 | import Link from "@tiptap/extension-link";
    8 | import type { JSONContent } from "@tiptap/core";
    9 | 
   10 | function isJsonDoc(v: unknown): v is JSONContent {
   11 |   return (
   12 |     !!v && typeof v === "object" && (v as { type?: unknown }).type === "doc"
   13 |   );
   14 | }
   15 | 
   16 | function makeLinkSafe(href: string): string | null {
   17 |   const h = (href ?? "").trim();
   18 |   if (!h) return null;
   19 | 
   20 |   // allow anchors and relative URLs
   21 |   if (h.startsWith("#") || h.startsWith("/")) return h;
   22 | 
   23 |   try {
   24 |     const u = new URL(h);
   25 |     const p = (u.protocol || "").toLowerCase();
   26 |     if (p === "http:" || p === "https:" || p === "mailto:") return u.toString();
   27 |     return null;
   28 |   } catch {
   29 |     return null;
   30 |   }
   31 | }
   32 | 
   33 | export default function TipTapReadOnly(props: { doc: unknown }) {
   34 |   const { doc } = props;
   35 | 
   36 |   const editor = useEditor({
   37 |     immediatelyRender: false,
   38 |     editable: false,
   39 |     extensions: [
   40 |       StarterKit.configure({
   41 |         heading: false,
   42 |         codeBlock: false,
   43 |         horizontalRule: false,
   44 |         link: false, // prevent duplicate name; we add Link below
   45 |       }),
   46 |       Link.configure({
   47 |         openOnClick: true,
   48 |         linkOnPaste: false,
   49 |         autolink: false,
   50 |         HTMLAttributes: {
   51 |           rel: "noopener noreferrer",
   52 |           target: "_blank",
   53 |         },
   54 |         validate: (href) => Boolean(makeLinkSafe(href)),
   55 |       }),
   56 |     ],
   57 |     content: "", // set via effect
   58 |     editorProps: {
   59 |       attributes: {
   60 |         class: "text-sm leading-relaxed",
   61 |       },
   62 |     },
   63 |   });
   64 | 
   65 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   66 |     if (!editor) return;
   67 | 
   68 |     if (!isJsonDoc(doc)) {
   69 |       editor.commands.setContent("", { emitUpdate: false });
   70 |       return;
   71 |     }
   72 | 
   73 |     editor.commands.setContent(doc, { emitUpdate: false });
   74 |   }, [editor, doc]);
   75 | 
   76 |   if (!isJsonDoc(doc) || !editor) return null;
   77 | 
   78 |   return <EditorContent editor={editor} />;
   79 | }

    5 | export function reorderRootsPinnedFirst(
    6 |   roots: Array<{ rootId: string; comments: CommentDTO[] }>,
    7 |   pinnedCommentId: string | null,
    8 | ) {
    9 |   const pid = (pinnedCommentId ?? "").trim();
   10 |   if (!pid) return roots;
   11 | 
   12 |   const idx = roots.findIndex((r) =>
   13 |     (r.comments ?? []).some((c) => c.id === pid),
   14 |   );
   15 |   if (idx <= 0) return roots;
   16 | 
   17 |   const pinned = roots[idx];
   18 |   const rest = roots.slice(0, idx).concat(roots.slice(idx + 1));
   19 |   return [pinned, ...rest];
   20 | }
   21 | 
   22 | export function parseHash(): {
   23 |   lineKey?: string;
   24 |   commentId?: string;
   25 |   rootId?: string;
   26 | } {
   27 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return {};
   28 |   const raw = (window.location.hash ?? "").replace(/^#/, "").trim();
   29 |   if (!raw) return {};
   30 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(raw);
   31 |   const lineKey = (sp.get("l") ?? "").trim();
   32 |   const commentId = (sp.get("c") ?? "").trim();
   33 |   const rootId = (sp.get("root") ?? "").trim();
   34 |   return {
   35 |     lineKey: lineKey || undefined,
   36 |     commentId: commentId || undefined,
   37 |     rootId: rootId || undefined,
   38 |   };
   39 | }
   40 | 
   41 | export function cueGroupKey(lyrics: LyricsApiOk, lineKey: string): string {
   42 |   const lk = (lineKey ?? "").trim();
   43 |   if (!lk) return "";
   44 |   const mapped = lyrics.groupMap?.[lk]?.canonicalGroupKey;
   45 |   if (typeof mapped === "string" && mapped.trim()) return mapped.trim();
   46 |   const cue = (lyrics.cues ?? []).find((x) => x.lineKey === lk);
   47 |   const groupKey = cue?.canonicalGroupKey;
   48 |   return typeof groupKey === "string" ? groupKey.trim() : "";
   49 | }
   50 | 
   51 | export function isSameGroup(a: string, b: string): boolean {
   52 |   const aa = (a ?? "").trim();
   53 |   const bb = (b ?? "").trim();
   54 |   return !!aa && !!bb && aa === bb;
   55 | }
   56 | 
   57 | export function cueCanonicalGroupKey(lyrics: LyricsApiOk, cue: LyricCue): string {
   58 |   return (cue.canonicalGroupKey ?? cueGroupKey(lyrics, cue.lineKey) ?? "").trim();
   59 | }
   60 | 
   61 | export function isTipTapDoc(
   62 |   value: unknown,
   63 | ): value is { type: "doc"; content?: unknown[] } {
   64 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
   65 |   const obj = value as { type?: unknown; content?: unknown };
   66 |   if (obj.type !== "doc") return false;
   67 |   if (typeof obj.content === "undefined") return true;
   68 |   return Array.isArray(obj.content);
   69 | }
   70 | 
   71 | export function medalTier(
   72 |   votes: number,
   73 | ): "copper" | "gold" | "adamantium" {
   74 |   const count = Math.max(0, votes || 0);
   75 |   if (count >= 7) return "adamantium";
   76 |   if (count >= 3) return "gold";
   77 |   return "copper";
   78 | }
   79 | 
   80 | export function medalClassForTier(
   81 |   tier: "copper" | "gold" | "adamantium",
   82 | ): string {
   83 |   if (tier === "copper") return "text-[#b87333]";
   84 |   if (tier === "gold") return "text-[#f5d062]";
   85 |   return "afMedalAdamantium";
   86 | }
   87 | 
   88 | export function rootTopScore(root: {
   89 |   rootId: string;
   90 |   comments: CommentDTO[];
   91 | }) {
   92 |   return (root.comments ?? []).reduce(
   93 |     (sum, comment) => sum + (comment.voteCount ?? 0),
   94 |     0,
   95 |   );
   96 | }
   97 | 
   98 | export function rootRecentTs(root: {
   99 |   rootId: string;
  100 |   comments: CommentDTO[];
  101 | }) {
  102 |   let best = 0;
  103 |   for (const comment of root.comments ?? []) {
  104 |     const timestamp = Date.parse((comment.editedAt ?? comment.createdAt) as string);
  105 |     if (!Number.isNaN(timestamp)) best = Math.max(best, timestamp);
  106 |   }
  107 |   return best;
  108 | }
  109 | 
  110 | export function formatAgo(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  111 |   const timestamp = Date.parse((iso ?? "") as string);
  112 |   if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp)) return "";
  113 | 
  114 |   const now = Date.now();
  115 |   const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - timestamp) / 1000));
  116 | 
  117 |   if (seconds < 60) return "just now";
  118 |   const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  119 |   if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
  120 |   const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  121 |   if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
  122 |   const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
  123 |   if (days < 7) return `${days}d ago`;
  124 |   const weeks = Math.floor(days / 7);
  125 |   if (weeks < 52) return `${weeks}w ago`;
  126 |   const years = Math.floor(days / 365);
  127 |   return `${Math.max(1, years)}y ago`;
  128 | }

    9 |   CommentEditErr,
   10 |   CommentEditOk,
   11 |   CommentPostOk,
   12 |   EditDraft,
   13 |   IdentityDTO,
   14 |   ReplyDraft,
   15 |   ReportDraft,
   16 |   ReportErr,
   17 |   ReportOk,
   18 |   ThreadApiErr,
   19 |   ThreadApiOk,
   20 |   ThreadSort,
   21 |   VoteErr,
   22 |   VoteOk,
   23 | } from "../exegesisTypes";
   24 | import type { ExegesisSelectedLine } from "./useExegesisHashState";
   25 | import {
   26 |   reorderRootsPinnedFirst,
   27 |   rootRecentTs,
   28 |   rootTopScore,
   29 | } from "../exegesisUi";
   30 | import { useBadgeAwardOverlay } from "@/app/home/badges/BadgeAwardOverlayProvider";
   31 | import { normalizeBadgeAwardNotices } from "@/app/home/badges/badgeAwardTypes";
   32 | 
   33 | export default function useExegesisThread(props: {
   34 |   recordingId: string;
   35 |   lyricsVersion: string | null | undefined;
   36 |   selected: ExegesisSelectedLine | null;
   37 |   sort: ThreadSort;
   38 |   userId: string | null | undefined;
   39 |   authLoaded: boolean;
   40 |   inlineGateOpen: boolean;
   41 |   applyGateResult: (res: GateResult, opts?: { dismissible?: boolean }) => void;
   42 |   onAuthChangedClearGate: () => void;
   43 |   setHash: (next: {
   44 |     lineKey?: string;
   45 |     commentId?: string;
   46 |     rootId?: string;
   47 |   }) => void;
   48 |   pendingScrollCommentIdRef: React.MutableRefObject<string>;
   49 |   focusedRootId: string;
   50 |   draft: string;
   51 |   draftDoc: unknown | null;
   52 |   setDraft: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
   53 |   setDraftDoc: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<unknown | null>>;
   54 |   setPosting: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
   55 |   replyByCommentId: Record<string, ReplyDraft>;
   56 |   setReplyByCommentId: React.Dispatch<
   57 |     React.SetStateAction<Record<string, ReplyDraft>>
   58 |   >;
   59 |   editByCommentId: Record<string, EditDraft>;
   60 |   setEditByCommentId: React.Dispatch<
   61 |     React.SetStateAction<Record<string, EditDraft>>
   62 |   >;
   63 |   reportByCommentId: Record<string, ReportDraft>;
   64 |   setReportByCommentId: React.Dispatch<
   65 |     React.SetStateAction<Record<string, ReportDraft>>
   66 |   >;
   67 |   claimName: string;
   68 |   setClaimName: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
   69 |   setClaimOpen: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
   70 |   setClaimErr: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
   71 |   setClaimBusy: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
   72 | }) {
   73 |   const {
   74 |     recordingId,
   75 |     lyricsVersion,
   76 |     selected,
   77 |     sort,
   78 |     userId,
   79 |     authLoaded,
   80 |     inlineGateOpen,
   81 |     applyGateResult,
   82 |     onAuthChangedClearGate,
   83 |     setHash,
   84 |     pendingScrollCommentIdRef,
   85 |     focusedRootId,
   86 |     draft,
   87 |     draftDoc,
   88 |     setDraft,
   89 |     setDraftDoc,
   90 |     setPosting,
   91 |     replyByCommentId,
   92 |     setReplyByCommentId,
   93 |     editByCommentId,
   94 |     setEditByCommentId,
   95 |     reportByCommentId,
   96 |     setReportByCommentId,
   97 |     claimName,
   98 |     setClaimName,
   99 |     setClaimOpen,
  100 |     setClaimErr,
  101 |     setClaimBusy,
  102 |   } = props;
  103 | 
  104 |   const [thread, setThread] = React.useState<ThreadApiOk | null>(null);
  105 |   const [threadErr, setThreadErr] = React.useState<string>("");
  106 |   const [threadLoading, setThreadLoading] = React.useState<boolean>(false);
  107 |   const [threadLoadedKey, setThreadLoadedKey] = React.useState<string>("");
  108 |   const { announceBadges } = useBadgeAwardOverlay();
  109 | 
  110 |   const isAnon = authLoaded ? !userId : false;
  111 |   const viewerKey = userId ?? "anon";
  112 | 
  113 |   const threadWantedCoreKey = React.useMemo(() => {
  114 |     const lk = (selected?.lineKey ?? "").trim();
  115 |     const gk = (selected?.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  116 |     if (!recordingId || !lk) return "";
  117 |     return `${recordingId}::${lk}::${gk}::${viewerKey}`;
  118 |   }, [recordingId, selected?.lineKey, selected?.groupKey, viewerKey]);
  119 | 
  120 |   const threadWantedFetchKey = threadWantedCoreKey;
  121 | 
  122 |   const threadUI =
  123 |     thread &&
  124 |     threadLoadedKey &&
  125 |     threadWantedCoreKey &&
  126 |     threadLoadedKey === threadWantedCoreKey
  127 |       ? thread
  128 |       : null;
  129 | 
  130 |   const shouldShowInitialShimmer =
  131 |     Boolean(threadWantedCoreKey) && !threadUI && !threadErr && threadLoading;
  132 | 
  133 |   const viewerMemberId =
  134 |     threadUI?.viewer?.kind === "member" ? threadUI.viewer.memberId : "";
  135 | 
  136 |   const viewerIdentity: IdentityDTO | undefined = viewerMemberId
  137 |     ? threadUI?.identities?.[viewerMemberId]
  138 |     : undefined;
  139 | 
  140 |   const meta = threadUI?.meta ?? null;
  141 |   const isLocked = Boolean(meta?.locked);
  142 | 
  143 |   const canVote =
  144 |     threadUI?.viewer?.kind === "member"
  145 |       ? threadUI.viewer.cap.canVote && !isLocked
  146 |       : false;
  147 | 
  148 |   const canReport =
  149 |     threadUI?.viewer?.kind === "member" ? threadUI.viewer.cap.canReport : false;
  150 | 
  151 |   const canPost =
  152 |     threadUI?.viewer?.kind === "member" ? threadUI.viewer.cap.canPost : false;
  153 | 
  154 |   const canClaimName =
  155 |     threadUI?.viewer?.kind === "member"
  156 |       ? threadUI.viewer.cap.canClaimName
  157 |       : false;
  158 | 
  159 |   const rootsForRender = React.useMemo(() => {
  160 |     const roots = [...(threadUI?.roots ?? [])];
  161 |     roots.sort((a, b) => {
  162 |       if (sort === "recent") return rootRecentTs(b) - rootRecentTs(a);
  163 |       return rootTopScore(b) - rootTopScore(a);
  164 |     });
  165 |     const pinnedId = meta?.pinnedCommentId ?? null;
  166 |     return reorderRootsPinnedFirst(roots, pinnedId);
  167 |   }, [threadUI?.roots, meta?.pinnedCommentId, sort]);
  168 | 
  169 |   const threadKey = thread
  170 |     ? `${thread.recordingId}::${thread.groupKey}::${thread.meta?.commentCount ?? 0}`
  171 |     : "";
  172 | 
  173 |   const prevUserIdRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
  174 | 
  175 |   const announceAwardPayload = React.useCallback(
  176 |     (payload: unknown) => {
  177 |       const badges = normalizeBadgeAwardNotices(payload);
  178 |       if (badges.length === 0) return;
  179 |       announceBadges(badges);
  180 |     },
  181 |     [announceBadges],
  182 |   );
  183 | 
  184 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  185 |     const prev = prevUserIdRef.current;
  186 |     const next = userId ?? null;
  187 |     prevUserIdRef.current = next;
  188 | 
  189 |     if (prev === next) return;
  190 | 
  191 |     setThreadLoadedKey("");
  192 |     setThreadErr("");
  193 |     setThread(null);
  194 |     onAuthChangedClearGate();
  195 |   }, [userId, onAuthChangedClearGate]);
  196 | 
  197 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  198 |     let alive = true;
  199 | 
  200 |     async function run() {
  201 |       if (!selected?.lineKey) return;
  202 |       if (inlineGateOpen) return;
  203 | 
  204 |       const wantedFetch = threadWantedFetchKey;
  205 |       const wantedCore = threadWantedCoreKey;
  206 |       if (!wantedFetch || !wantedCore) return;
  207 | 
  208 |       setThreadLoading(true);
  209 |       setThreadErr("");
  210 | 
  211 |       const gk = (selected.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  212 | 
  213 |       const url =
  214 |         `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  215 |         (gk
  216 |           ? `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(gk)}&lineKey=${encodeURIComponent(
  217 |               selected.lineKey,
  218 |             )}`
  219 |           : `&lineKey=${encodeURIComponent(selected.lineKey)}`);
  220 | 
  221 |       try {
  222 |         const r = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  223 |         const j = (await r.json()) as ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr;
  224 |         if (!alive) return;
  225 | 
  226 |         if (!j.ok) {
  227 |           setThread((prev) => (prev ? prev : null));
  228 | 
  229 |           if (j.gate) {
  230 |             const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  231 |               payload: j.gate,
  232 |               attempt: { verb: "readFullThread", domain: "exegesis" },
  233 |               isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  234 |               intent: "passive",
  235 |             });
  236 | 
  237 |             if (res.ok) {
  238 |               setThreadErr(j.error || "Failed to load thread.");
  239 |               return;
  240 |             }
  241 | 
  242 |             applyGateResult(res);
  243 |             setThreadErr("");
  244 |             return;
  245 |           }
  246 | 
  247 |           setThreadErr(j.error || "Failed to load thread.");
  248 |           return;
  249 |         }
  250 | 
  251 |         setThread(j);
  252 |         setThreadErr("");
  253 |         setThreadLoadedKey(wantedCore);
  254 |       } catch {
  255 |         if (!alive) return;
  256 |         setThread((prev) => (prev ? prev : null));
  257 |         setThreadErr("Failed to load thread.");
  258 |       } finally {
  259 |         if (!alive) return;
  260 |         setThreadLoading(false);
  261 |       }
  262 |     }
  263 | 
  264 |     void run();
  265 |     return () => {
  266 |       alive = false;
  267 |     };
  268 |   }, [
  269 |     recordingId,
  270 |     selected?.lineKey,
  271 |     selected?.groupKey,
  272 |     threadWantedFetchKey,
  273 |     threadWantedCoreKey,
  274 |     userId,
  275 |     inlineGateOpen,
  276 |     applyGateResult,
  277 |   ]);
  278 | 
  279 |   function closeEdit(commentId: string) {
  280 |     setEditByCommentId((prev) => {
  281 |       if (!prev[commentId]) return prev;
  282 |       const next = { ...prev };
  283 |       delete next[commentId];
  284 |       return next;
  285 |     });
  286 |   }
  287 | 
  288 |   async function submitReport(commentId: string) {
  289 |     if (!canReport) return;
  290 | 
  291 |     const draft0 = reportByCommentId[commentId];
  292 |     if (!draft0) return;
  293 | 
  294 |     const category = (draft0.category ?? "").trim();
  295 |     const reason = (draft0.reason ?? "").trim();
  296 | 
  297 |     setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  298 |       ...prev,
  299 |       [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: true, err: "" },
  300 |     }));
  301 | 
  302 |     try {
  303 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/report", {
  304 |         method: "POST",
  305 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  306 |         body: JSON.stringify({ commentId, category, reason }),
  307 |       });
  308 | 
  309 |       const j = (await r.json()) as ReportOk | ReportErr;
  310 | 
  311 |       if (!j.ok) {
  312 |         setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  313 |           ...prev,
  314 |           [commentId]: {
  315 |             ...draft0,
  316 |             busy: false,
  317 |             err: j.error || "Report failed.",
  318 |           },
  319 |         }));
  320 |         return;
  321 |       }
  322 | 
  323 |       setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  324 |         ...prev,
  325 |         [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: false, done: true, err: "" },
  326 |       }));
  327 |     } catch {
  328 |       setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  329 |         ...prev,
  330 |         [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: false, err: "Report failed." },
  331 |       }));
  332 |     }
  333 |   }
  334 | 
  335 |   async function submitClaimName() {
  336 |     if (!canClaimName) return;
  337 | 
  338 |     const name = claimName.trim();
  339 |     if (!name) return;
  340 | 
  341 |     setClaimBusy(true);
  342 |     setClaimErr("");
  343 | 
  344 |     try {
  345 |       const r = await fetch("/api/identity/claim-name", {
  346 |         method: "POST",
  347 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  348 |         body: JSON.stringify({ publicName: name }),
  349 |       });
  350 | 
  351 |       const j = (await r.json()) as
  352 |         | { ok: true; identity: IdentityDTO }
  353 |         | { ok: false; error: string; code?: string };
  354 | 
  355 |       if (!j.ok) {
  356 |         setClaimErr(j.error || "Failed to claim name.");
  357 |         return;
  358 |       }
  359 | 
  360 |       setThread((prev) => {
  361 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  362 | 
  363 |         return {
  364 |           ...prev,
  365 |           identities: {
  366 |             ...prev.identities,
  367 |             [j.identity.memberId]: j.identity,
  368 |           },
  369 |         };
  370 |       });
  371 | 
  372 |       setClaimErr("");
  373 |       setClaimOpen(false);
  374 |       setClaimName("");
  375 |     } catch {
  376 |       setClaimErr("Failed to claim name.");
  377 |     } finally {
  378 |       setClaimBusy(false);
  379 |     }
  380 |   }
  381 | 
  382 |   async function submitEdit(comment: CommentDTO) {
  383 |     if (!canPost) {
  384 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to edit.");
  385 |       return;
  386 |     }
  387 |     if (isLocked) {
  388 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  389 |       return;
  390 |     }
  391 |     if (!thread) {
  392 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  393 |       return;
  394 |     }
  395 |     if (!viewerMemberId || comment.createdByMemberId !== viewerMemberId) {
  396 |       setThreadErr("You can only edit your own comments.");
  397 |       return;
  398 |     }
  399 |     if (comment.status !== "live") {
  400 |       setThreadErr("Only live comments can be edited.");
  401 |       return;
  402 |     }
  403 | 
  404 |     const draft0 = editByCommentId[comment.id];
  405 |     if (!draft0 || !draft0.open) return;
  406 | 
  407 |     const text = (draft0.plain ?? "").trim();
  408 |     if (!text) {
  409 |       setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  410 |         ...prev,
  411 |         [comment.id]: { ...draft0, err: "Edit is empty." },
  412 |       }));
  413 |       return;
  414 |     }
  415 | 
  416 |     setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  417 |       ...prev,
  418 |       [comment.id]: { ...draft0, posting: true, err: "" },
  419 |     }));
  420 |     setThreadErr("");
  421 | 
  422 |     const doc =
  423 |       draft0.doc ??
  424 |       ({
  425 |         type: "doc",
  426 |         content: [{ type: "paragraph", content: [{ type: "text", text }] }],
  427 |       } as const);
  428 | 
  429 |     try {
  430 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment/edit", {
  431 |         method: "POST",
  432 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  433 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  434 |           commentId: comment.id,
  435 |           bodyPlain: text,
  436 |           bodyRich: doc,
  437 |         }),
  438 |       });
  439 | 
  440 |       const j = (await r.json()) as CommentEditOk | CommentEditErr;
  441 | 
  442 |       if (!j.ok) {
  443 |         setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  444 |           ...prev,
  445 |           [comment.id]: {
  446 |             ...draft0,
  447 |             posting: false,
  448 |             err: j.error || "Edit failed.",
  449 |           },
  450 |         }));
  451 |         return;
  452 |       }
  453 | 
  454 |       setThread((prev) => {
  455 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  456 | 
  457 |         const roots = (prev.roots ?? []).map((root) => ({
  458 |           ...root,
  459 |           comments: (root.comments ?? []).map((x) =>
  460 |             x.id === j.comment.id ? { ...x, ...j.comment } : x,
  461 |           ),
  462 |         }));
  463 | 
  464 |         return { ...prev, roots, meta: j.meta };
  465 |       });
  466 | 
  467 |       closeEdit(comment.id);
  468 |     } finally {
  469 |       setEditByCommentId((prev) => {
  470 |         const cur = prev[comment.id];
  471 |         if (!cur) return prev;
  472 |         if (!cur.posting) return prev;
  473 |         return { ...prev, [comment.id]: { ...cur, posting: false } };
  474 |       });
  475 |     }
  476 |   }
  477 | 
  478 |   async function postComment() {
  479 |     if (!selected) return;
  480 |     if (!canPost) {
  481 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to post.");
  482 |       return;
  483 |     }
  484 | 
  485 |     if (thread?.meta?.locked) {
  486 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  487 |       return;
  488 |     }
  489 | 
  490 |     const groupKey = (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  491 |     if (!groupKey) {
  492 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  493 |       return;
  494 |     }
  495 | 
  496 |     const text = draft.trim();
  497 |     if (!text) return;
  498 | 
  499 |     setPosting(true);
  500 |     setThreadErr("");
  501 | 
  502 |     try {
  503 |       const doc =
  504 |         draftDoc ??
  505 |         ({
  506 |           type: "doc",
  507 |           content: [
  508 |             {
  509 |               type: "paragraph",
  510 |               content: [{ type: "text", text }],
  511 |             },
  512 |           ],
  513 |         } as const);
  514 | 
  515 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment", {
  516 |         method: "POST",
  517 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  518 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  519 |           recordingId,
  520 |           lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  521 |           groupKey: (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim(),
  522 |           parentId: null,
  523 |           bodyPlain: text,
  524 |           bodyRich: doc,
  525 |           tMs: selected.tMs,
  526 |           lineTextSnapshot: selected.lineText,
  527 |           lyricsVersion: lyricsVersion ?? null,
  528 |         }),
  529 |       });
  530 | 
  531 |       const j = (await r.json()) as
  532 |         | (CommentPostOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  533 |         | {
  534 |             ok: false;
  535 |             error: string;
  536 |             gate?: ThreadApiErr["gate"];
  537 |             newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown;
  538 |           };
  539 | 
  540 |       if (!j.ok) {
  541 |         if (j.gate) {
  542 |           const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  543 |             payload: j.gate,
  544 |             attempt: { verb: "postComment", domain: "exegesis" },
  545 |             isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  546 |             intent: "explicit",
  547 |           });
  548 | 
  549 |           if (!res.ok) {
  550 |             applyGateResult(res);
  551 |             setThreadErr("");
  552 |             return;
  553 |           }
  554 |         }
  555 | 
  556 |         setThreadErr(j.error || "Failed to post comment.");
  557 |         return;
  558 |       }
  559 | 
  560 |       setDraft("");
  561 |       setDraftDoc(null);
  562 |       announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  563 | 
  564 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = j.comment.id;
  565 |       setHash({
  566 |         lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  567 |         commentId: j.comment.id,
  568 |         rootId: focusedRootId || undefined,
  569 |       });
  570 | 
  571 |       setThread((prev) => {
  572 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  573 |         if (
  574 |           prev.recordingId !== j.recordingId ||
  575 |           prev.groupKey !== j.groupKey
  576 |         ) {
  577 |           return prev;
  578 |         }
  579 | 
  580 |         const newRoot = { rootId: j.comment.rootId, comments: [j.comment] };
  581 |         return {
  582 |           ...prev,
  583 |           meta: j.meta,
  584 |           roots: [newRoot, ...prev.roots],
  585 |           identities: { ...prev.identities, ...j.identities },
  586 |         };
  587 |       });
  588 | 
  589 |       const url =
  590 |         `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  591 |         `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(groupKey)}`;
  592 | 
  593 |       fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" })
  594 |         .then((r2) => r2.json())
  595 |         .then((jj: ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr) => {
  596 |           if (jj && (jj as ThreadApiOk).ok) {
  597 |             setThread(jj as ThreadApiOk);
  598 |             setThreadErr("");
  599 |           }
  600 |         })
  601 |         .catch(() => {});
  602 |     } finally {
  603 |       setPosting(false);
  604 |     }
  605 |   }
  606 | 
  607 |   async function postReply(parentComment: CommentDTO) {
  608 |     if (!selected) return;
  609 |     if (!canPost) {
  610 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to post.");
  611 |       return;
  612 |     }
  613 |     if (isLocked) {
  614 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  615 |       return;
  616 |     }
  617 |     if (!thread) {
  618 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  619 |       return;
  620 |     }
  621 | 
  622 |     const parentId = parentComment.id;
  623 |     const draft0 = replyByCommentId[parentId];
  624 |     if (!draft0 || !draft0.open) return;
  625 | 
  626 |     const text = (draft0.plain ?? "").trim();
  627 |     if (!text) {
  628 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  629 |         ...prev,
  630 |         [parentId]: { ...draft0, err: "Reply is empty." },
  631 |       }));
  632 |       return;
  633 |     }
  634 | 
  635 |     if ((parentComment.depth ?? 0) + 1 > 6) {
  636 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  637 |         ...prev,
  638 |         [parentId]: { ...draft0, err: "Thread depth limit reached." },
  639 |       }));
  640 |       return;
  641 |     }
  642 | 
  643 |     setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  644 |       ...prev,
  645 |       [parentId]: { ...draft0, posting: true, err: "" },
  646 |     }));
  647 |     setThreadErr("");
  648 | 
  649 |     const groupKey = (thread.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  650 | 
  651 |     try {
  652 |       const doc =
  653 |         draft0.doc ??
  654 |         ({
  655 |           type: "doc",
  656 |           content: [
  657 |             {
  658 |               type: "paragraph",
  659 |               content: [{ type: "text", text }],
  660 |             },
  661 |           ],
  662 |         } as const);
  663 | 
  664 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment", {
  665 |         method: "POST",
  666 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  667 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  668 |           recordingId,
  669 |           lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  670 |           groupKey,
  671 |           parentId,
  672 |           bodyPlain: text,
  673 |           bodyRich: doc,
  674 |           tMs: selected.tMs,
  675 |           lineTextSnapshot: selected.lineText,
  676 |           lyricsVersion: lyricsVersion ?? null,
  677 |         }),
  678 |       });
  679 | 
  680 |       const j = (await r.json()) as
  681 |         | (CommentPostOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  682 |         | {
  683 |             ok: false;
  684 |             error: string;
  685 |             gate?: ThreadApiErr["gate"];
  686 |             newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown;
  687 |           };
  688 | 
  689 |       if (!j.ok) {
  690 |         if (j.gate) {
  691 |           const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  692 |             payload: j.gate,
  693 |             attempt: { verb: "postComment", domain: "exegesis" },
  694 |             isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  695 |             intent: "explicit",
  696 |           });
  697 | 
  698 |           if (!res.ok) {
  699 |             applyGateResult(res);
  700 |             setThreadErr("");
  701 |             return;
  702 |           }
  703 |         }
  704 | 
  705 |         setThreadErr(j.error || "Reply failed.");
  706 |         return;
  707 |       }
  708 | 
  709 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  710 |         ...prev,
  711 |         [parentId]: {
  712 |           ...draft0,
  713 |           posting: false,
  714 |           open: false,
  715 |           plain: "",

  718 |         },
  719 |       }));
  720 |       announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  721 | 
  722 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = j.comment.id;
  723 |       setHash({
  724 |         lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  725 |         commentId: j.comment.id,
  726 |         rootId: focusedRootId || undefined,
  727 |       });
  728 | 
  729 |       setThread((prev) => {
  730 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  731 |         if (
  732 |           prev.recordingId !== j.recordingId ||
  733 |           prev.groupKey !== j.groupKey
  734 |         ) {
  735 |           return prev;
  736 |         }
  737 | 
  738 |         const roots = (prev.roots ?? []).map((root) => {
  739 |           if (root.rootId !== j.comment.rootId) return root;
  740 |           return { ...root, comments: [...(root.comments ?? []), j.comment] };
  741 |         });
  742 | 
  743 |         const found = roots.some((r0) => r0.rootId === j.comment.rootId);
  744 |         const roots2 = found
  745 |           ? roots
  746 |           : roots.concat([{ rootId: j.comment.rootId, comments: [j.comment] }]);
  747 | 
  748 |         return {
  749 |           ...prev,

  753 |         };
  754 |       });
  755 | 
  756 |       const url =

  764 |             setThread(jj as ThreadApiOk);
  765 |             setThreadErr("");
  766 |           }
  767 |         })
  768 |         .catch(() => {});
  769 |     } finally {
  770 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => {
  771 |         const cur = prev[parentId];
  772 |         if (!cur) return prev;
  773 |         if (!cur.posting) return prev;
  774 |         return { ...prev, [parentId]: { ...cur, posting: false } };
  775 |       });
  776 |     }
  777 |   }
  778 | 
  779 |   async function toggleVote(commentId: string) {
  780 |     if (!thread) return;
  781 | 
  782 |     if (!canVote) {
  783 |       setThreadErr(
  784 |         thread.viewer.kind === "anon"
  785 |           ? "Sign in to vote."
  786 |           : "Friend tier or higher required to vote.",
  787 |       );
  788 |       return;
  789 |     }
  790 | 
  791 |     setThreadErr("");
  792 | 
  793 |     setThread((prev) => {
  794 |       if (!prev) return prev;
  795 |       const roots = prev.roots.map((r) => ({
  796 |         ...r,
  797 |         comments: r.comments.map((c) => {
  798 |           if (c.id !== commentId) return c;
  799 |           const nextHas = !c.viewerHasVoted;
  800 |           const nextCount = Math.max(0, c.voteCount + (nextHas ? 1 : -1));
  801 |           return { ...c, viewerHasVoted: nextHas, voteCount: nextCount };
  802 |         }),
  803 |       }));
  804 |       return { ...prev, roots };
  805 |     });
  806 | 
  807 |     const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/vote", {
  808 |       method: "POST",
  809 |       headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  810 |       body: JSON.stringify({ commentId }),
  811 |     });
  812 | 
  813 |     const j = (await r.json()) as
  814 |       | (VoteOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  815 |       | (VoteErr & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown });
  816 | 
  817 |     if (!j.ok) {
  818 |       if (j.gate) {
  819 |         const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  820 |           payload: j.gate,
  821 |           attempt: { verb: "vote", domain: "exegesis" },
  822 |           isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  823 |           intent: "explicit",
  824 |         });
  825 | 
  826 |         if (!res.ok) {
  827 |           applyGateResult(res);
  828 |           setThreadErr("");
  829 |           return;
  830 |         }
  831 |       }
  832 | 
  833 |       setThreadErr(j.error || "Vote failed.");
  834 | 
  835 |       if (selected) {
  836 |         const gk = (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  837 |         const url =
  838 |           `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  839 |           (gk
  840 |             ? `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(gk)}`
  841 |             : `&lineKey=${encodeURIComponent(selected.lineKey)}`);
  842 |         const rr = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  843 |         const jj = (await rr.json()) as ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr;
  844 |         if (jj.ok) {
  845 |           setThread(jj);
  846 |           setThreadErr("");
  847 |         }
  848 |       }
  849 |       return;
  850 |     }
  851 | 
  852 |     setThread((prev) => {
  853 |       if (!prev) return prev;
  854 |       const roots = prev.roots.map((r0) => ({
  855 |         ...r0,
  856 |         comments: r0.comments.map((c) =>
  857 |           c.id === j.commentId
  858 |             ? { ...c, viewerHasVoted: j.viewerHasVoted, voteCount: j.voteCount }
  859 |             : c,
  860 |         ),
  861 |       }));
  862 |       return { ...prev, roots };
  863 |     });
  864 | 
  865 |     announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  866 |   }
  867 |

   61 | async function requireCanPost(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   62 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
   63 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
   64 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
   65 |   ]);
   66 | }
   67 | 
   68 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   69 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   70 | 
   71 |   let raw: unknown;
   72 |   try {
   73 |     raw = await req.json();
   74 |   } catch {
   75 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   76 |       ok: false,
   77 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   78 |     });
   79 |   }
   80 | 
   81 |   const b = bodyRecord(raw);
   82 |   if (!b) {
   83 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   84 |       ok: false,
   85 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   86 |     });
   87 |   }
   88 | 
   89 |   const commentId = normString(b.commentId);
   90 |   if (!commentId || !isUuid(commentId)) {
   91 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   92 |       ok: false,
   93 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
   94 |     });
   95 |   }
   96 | 
   97 |   if (!("bodyRich" in b)) {
   98 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   99 |       ok: false,
  100 |       error: "Missing bodyRich.",
  101 |     });
  102 |   }
  103 |   const bodyRichInput: unknown = b.bodyRich ?? null;
  104 | 
  105 |   const v = validateAndSanitizeTipTapDoc(bodyRichInput);
  106 |   if (!v.ok) {
  107 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, { ok: false, error: v.error });
  108 |   }
  109 | 
  110 |   const bodyPlain = v.plain;
  111 | 
  112 |   let bodyRichJson = "";
  113 |   try {
  114 |     bodyRichJson = JSON.stringify(v.doc);
  115 |   } catch {
  116 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  117 |       ok: false,
  118 |       error: "Invalid bodyRich.",
  119 |     });
  120 |   }
  121 | 
  122 |   if (bodyRichJson.length > 200_000) {
  123 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  124 |       ok: false,
  125 |       error: "bodyRich too large.",
  126 |     });
  127 |   }
  128 | 
  129 |   const memberId = await requireExegesisMemberId();
  130 |   if (!memberId) {
  131 |     return gateError(req, {
  132 |       correlationId,
  133 |       status: 401,
  134 |       domain: "exegesis",
  135 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  136 |       action: "login",
  137 |       message: "Sign in to edit a comment.",
  138 |     });
  139 |   }
  140 | 
  141 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  142 |     return gateError(req, {
  143 |       correlationId,
  144 |       status: 403,
  145 |       domain: "exegesis",
  146 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  147 |       action: "wait",
  148 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  149 |     });
  150 |   }
  151 | 
  152 |   const canPost = await requireCanPost(memberId);
  153 |   if (!canPost) {
  154 |     return gateError(req, {
  155 |       correlationId,
  156 |       status: 403,
  157 |       domain: "exegesis",
  158 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  159 |       action: "subscribe",
  160 |       message: "Editing requires Patron or Partner.",
  161 |     });
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   try {
  165 |     const q = await sql<{
  166 |       guard_err: string | null;
  167 |       id: string | null;
  168 |       track_id: string | null;
  169 |       group_key: string | null;
  170 |       line_key: string | null;
  171 |       parent_id: string | null;
  172 |       root_id: string | null;
  173 |       depth: number | null;
  174 |       body_rich: unknown | null;
  175 |       body_plain: string | null;
  176 |       t_ms: number | null;
  177 |       line_text_snapshot: string | null;
  178 |       lyrics_version: string | null;
  179 |       created_by_member_id: string | null;
  180 |       status: "live" | "hidden" | "deleted" | null;
  181 |       created_at: string | null;
  182 |       edited_at: string | null;
  183 |       edit_count: number | null;
  184 |       vote_count: number | null;
  185 |       meta_track_id: string | null;
  186 |       meta_group_key: string | null;
  187 |       meta_pinned_comment_id: string | null;
  188 |       meta_locked: boolean | null;
  189 |       meta_comment_count: number | null;
  190 |       meta_last_activity_at: string | null;
  191 |       meta_created_at: string | null;
  192 |       meta_updated_at: string | null;
  193 |     }>`
  194 |     with
  195 | one as (select 1 as one),
  196 | params as (
  197 |   select
  198 |     ${commentId}::uuid     as comment_id,
  199 |     ${memberId}::uuid      as member_id,
  200 |     ${bodyRichJson}::jsonb as body_rich,
  201 |     ${bodyPlain}::text     as body_plain
  202 | ),
  203 | target as (
  204 |   select
  205 |     c.id,
  206 |     c.track_id,
  207 |     c.group_key,
  208 |     c.status,
  209 |     c.created_by_member_id
  210 |   from exegesis_comment c
  211 |   join params p on c.id = p.comment_id
  212 |   limit 1
  213 | ),
  214 | meta_base as (
  215 |   insert into exegesis_thread_meta (track_id, group_key)
  216 |   select t.track_id, t.group_key
  217 |   from target t
  218 |   on conflict (track_id, group_key) do nothing
  219 |   returning track_id, group_key, pinned_comment_id, locked,
  220 |             comment_count, last_activity_at, created_at, updated_at
  221 | ),
  222 | meta_existing as (
  223 |   select m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  224 |          m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  225 |   from exegesis_thread_meta m
  226 |   join target t on t.track_id = m.track_id and t.group_key = m.group_key
  227 |   limit 1
  228 | ),
  229 | meta_pre as (
  230 |   select * from meta_base
  231 |   union all
  232 |   select * from meta_existing
  233 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_base)
  234 | ),
  235 | guard_row as (
  236 |   select
  237 |     case
  238 |       when t.id is null then 'NOT_FOUND'
  239 |       when mp.locked then 'LOCKED'
  240 |       when t.status = 'deleted' then 'DELETED'
  241 |       when t.status = 'hidden' then 'HIDDEN'
  242 |       when t.created_by_member_id <> p.member_id then 'FORBIDDEN'
  243 |       else null
  244 |     end as err
  245 |   from one
  246 |   left join target t on true
  247 |   left join meta_pre mp on true
  248 |   left join params p on true
  249 | ),
  250 | upd as (
  251 |   update exegesis_comment c
  252 |   set
  253 |     body_rich = p.body_rich,
  254 |     body_plain = p.body_plain,
  255 |     edited_at = now(),
  256 |     edit_count = c.edit_count + 1
  257 |   from params p
  258 |   cross join guard_row g
  259 |   where c.id = p.comment_id
  260 |     and g.err is null
  261 |   returning
  262 |     c.id, c.track_id, c.group_key, c.line_key, c.parent_id, c.root_id, c.depth,
  263 |     c.body_rich, c.body_plain, c.t_ms, c.line_text_snapshot, c.lyrics_version,
  264 |     c.created_by_member_id, c.status::text as status,
  265 |     c.created_at, c.edited_at, c.edit_count, c.vote_count
  266 | ),
  267 | meta_upd as (
  268 |   update exegesis_thread_meta m
  269 |   set
  270 |     last_activity_at = now(),
  271 |     updated_at = now()
  272 |   where m.track_id = (select track_id from target)
  273 |     and m.group_key = (select group_key from target)
  274 |     and exists (select 1 from upd)
  275 |   returning m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  276 |             m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  277 | ),
  278 | meta_out as (
  279 |   select * from meta_upd
  280 |   union all
  281 |   select * from meta_pre
  282 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_upd)
  283 | )
  284 | select
  285 |   g.err as guard_err,
  286 |   u.id,
  287 |   u.track_id,
  288 |   u.group_key,
  289 |   u.line_key,
  290 |   u.parent_id,
  291 |   u.root_id,
  292 |   u.depth,
  293 |   u.body_rich,
  294 |   u.body_plain,
  295 |   u.t_ms,
  296 |   u.line_text_snapshot,
  297 |   u.lyrics_version,
  298 |   u.created_by_member_id,
  299 |   u.status,
  300 |   u.created_at,
  301 |   u.edited_at,
  302 |   u.edit_count,
  303 |   u.vote_count,
  304 |   m.track_id as meta_track_id,
  305 |   m.group_key as meta_group_key,
  306 |   m.pinned_comment_id as meta_pinned_comment_id,
  307 |   m.locked as meta_locked,
  308 |   m.comment_count as meta_comment_count,
  309 |   m.last_activity_at as meta_last_activity_at,
  310 |   m.created_at as meta_created_at,
  311 |   m.updated_at as meta_updated_at
  312 | from one
  313 | left join guard_row g on true
  314 | left join upd u on true
  315 | left join meta_out m on true
  316 | limit 1
  317 |     `;
  318 | 
  319 |     const row = q.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  320 |     if (!row) {
  321 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  322 |         ok: false,
  323 |         error: "No response row.",
  324 |       });
  325 |     }
  326 | 
  327 |     if (row.guard_err) {
  328 |       if (row.guard_err === "NOT_FOUND") {
  329 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  330 |           ok: false,
  331 |           error: "Comment not found.",
  332 |         });
  333 |       }
  334 |       if (row.guard_err === "LOCKED") {
  335 |         return gateError(req, {
  336 |           correlationId,
  337 |           status: 403,
  338 |           domain: "exegesis",
  339 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  340 |           action: "wait",
  341 |           message: "Thread is locked.",
  342 |         });
  343 |       }
  344 |       if (row.guard_err === "FORBIDDEN") {
  345 |         return gateError(req, {
  346 |           correlationId,
  347 |           status: 403,
  348 |           domain: "exegesis",
  349 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  350 |           action: "wait",
  351 |           message: "You can only edit your own comments.",
  352 |         });
  353 |       }
  354 |       if (row.guard_err === "DELETED") {
  355 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  356 |           ok: false,
  357 |           error: "Cannot edit a deleted comment.",
  358 |         });
  359 |       }
  360 |       if (row.guard_err === "HIDDEN") {
  361 |         return gateError(req, {
  362 |           correlationId,
  363 |           status: 403,
  364 |           domain: "exegesis",
  365 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  366 |           action: "wait",
  367 |           message: "Cannot edit a hidden comment.",
  368 |         });
  369 |       }
  370 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  371 |         ok: false,
  372 |         error: "Cannot edit comment.",
  373 |       });
  374 |     }
  375 | 
  376 |     if (!row.id || !row.track_id || !row.group_key) {
  377 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  378 |         ok: false,
  379 |         error: "Edit failed.",
  380 |       });
  381 |     }
  382 | 
  383 |     const comment: CommentDTO = {
  384 |       id: row.id,
  385 |       recordingId: row.track_id,
  386 |       groupKey: row.group_key,
  387 |       lineKey: row.line_key ?? "",
  388 |       parentId: row.parent_id ?? null,
  389 |       rootId: row.root_id ?? row.id,
  390 |       depth: Number(row.depth ?? 0),
  391 |       bodyRich: row.body_rich ?? {},
  392 |       bodyPlain: row.body_plain ?? "",
  393 |       tMs: row.t_ms ?? null,
  394 |       lineTextSnapshot: row.line_text_snapshot ?? "",
  395 |       lyricsVersion: row.lyrics_version ?? null,
  396 |       createdByMemberId: row.created_by_member_id ?? "",
  397 |       status: (row.status ?? "live") as "live" | "hidden" | "deleted",
  398 |       createdAt: row.created_at ?? "",
  399 |       editedAt: row.edited_at ?? null,
  400 |       editCount: Number(row.edit_count ?? 0),
  401 |       voteCount: Number(row.vote_count ?? 0),
  402 |       viewerHasVoted: false,
  403 |     };
  404 | 
  405 |     const meta: ThreadMetaDTO = {
  406 |       recordingId: String(row.meta_track_id ?? row.track_id),
  407 |       groupKey: String(row.meta_group_key ?? row.group_key),
  408 |       pinnedCommentId: row.meta_pinned_comment_id ?? null,
  409 |       locked: Boolean(row.meta_locked),

  412 |       createdAt: String(row.meta_created_at ?? ""),
  413 |       updatedAt: String(row.meta_updated_at ?? ""),
  414 |     };
  415 |
