   48 |   lineKey: string;
   49 |   parentId: string | null;
   50 |   rootId: string;
   51 |   depth: number;
   52 |   bodyRich: unknown;
   53 |   bodyPlain: string;
   54 |   tMs: number | null;
   55 |   lineTextSnapshot: string;
   56 |   lyricsVersion: string | null;
   57 |   createdByMemberId: string;
   58 |   status: "live" | "hidden" | "deleted";
   59 |   createdAt: string;
   60 |   editedAt: string | null;
   61 |   editCount: number;
   62 |   voteCount: number;
   63 |   viewerHasVoted: boolean;
   64 | };
   65 | 
   66 | type ThreadMetaDTO = {
   67 |   recordingId: string;
   68 |   groupKey: string;
   69 |   pinnedCommentId: string | null;
   70 |   locked: boolean;
   71 |   commentCount: number;
   72 |   lastActivityAt: string;
   73 |   createdAt: string;
   74 |   updatedAt: string;
   75 | };
   76 | 
   77 | type CommentContent = {
   78 |   bodyPlain: string;
   79 |   bodyRichJson: string;
   80 | };
   81 | 
   82 | type CommentCommandDraft = CommentContent & {
   83 |   recordingId: string;
   84 |   lineKey: string;
   85 |   groupKeyClient: string;
   86 |   parentId: string | null;
   87 |   lineTextSnapshot: string;
   88 |   lyricsVersion: string | null;
   89 |   tMs: number | null;
   90 | };
   91 | 
   92 | type CommentCommand = Omit<CommentCommandDraft, "groupKeyClient"> & {
   93 |   groupKey: string;
   94 | };
   95 | 
   96 | type ValidationResult<T> =
   97 |   | { ok: true; value: T }
   98 |   | { ok: false; status: number; error: string };
   99 | 
  100 | type PostingIdentity = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof ensureMemberIdentity>>;
  101 | 
  102 | type PostingAuthorityResult =
  103 |   | { ok: true; memberId: string; identity: PostingIdentity }
  104 |   | { ok: false; response: NextResponse };
  105 | 
  106 | type CommentInsertRow = {
  107 |   inserted_count: number;
  108 |   guard_err: string | null;
  109 |   id: string | null;
  110 |   track_id: string | null;
  111 |   group_key: string | null;
  112 |   line_key: string | null;
  113 |   parent_id: string | null;
  114 |   root_id: string | null;
  115 |   depth: number | null;
  116 |   body_rich: unknown | null;
  117 |   body_plain: string | null;
  118 |   t_ms: number | null;
  119 |   line_text_snapshot: string | null;
  120 |   lyrics_version: string | null;
  121 |   created_by_member_id: string | null;
  122 |   status: "live" | "hidden" | "deleted" | null;
  123 |   created_at: string | null;
  124 |   edited_at: string | null;
  125 |   edit_count: number | null;
  126 |   vote_count: number | null;
  127 |   meta_track_id: string;
  128 |   meta_group_key: string;
  129 |   meta_pinned_comment_id: string | null;
  130 |   meta_locked: boolean;
  131 |   meta_comment_count: number;
  132 |   meta_last_activity_at: string;
  133 |   meta_created_at: string;
  134 |   meta_updated_at: string;
  135 |   ident_member_id: string;
  136 |   ident_public_name_unlocked_at: string | null;
  137 |   ident_contribution_count: number;
  138 | };
  139 | 
  140 | type InsertedCommentRow = CommentInsertRow & {
  141 |   inserted_count: 1;
  142 |   id: string;
  143 |   track_id: string;
  144 |   group_key: string;
  145 |   line_key: string;
  146 |   root_id: string;
  147 |   depth: number;
  148 |   body_plain: string;
  149 |   line_text_snapshot: string;
  150 |   created_by_member_id: string;
  151 |   status: "live" | "hidden" | "deleted";
  152 |   created_at: string;
  153 |   edit_count: number;
  154 |   vote_count: number;
  155 | };
  156 | 
  157 | type InsertResolution =
  158 |   | { ok: true; row: InsertedCommentRow }
  159 |   | { ok: false; response: NextResponse };
  160 | 
  161 | const EMPTY_BODY_RICH_JSON = JSON.stringify({
  162 |   type: "doc",
  163 |   content: [{ type: "paragraph" }],
  164 | });
  165 | 
  166 | function jsonErr(correlationId: string, status: number, body: ApiErr) {
  167 |   return jsonExegesisErr(correlationId, status, body);
  168 | }
  169 | 
  170 | function validationError(
  171 |   status: number,
  172 |   error: string,
  173 | ): ValidationResult<never> {
  174 |   return { ok: false, status, error };
  175 | }
  176 | 
  177 | function clampInt(v: unknown, min: number, max: number): number | null {
  178 |   if (typeof v !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(v)) return null;
  179 |   const n = Math.trunc(v);
  180 |   if (n < min) return min;
  181 |   if (n > max) return max;
  182 |   return n;
  183 | }
  184 | 
  185 | function normNullableId(v: unknown): string | null {
  186 |   const value = normString(v);
  187 |   if (!value || value === "null" || value === "undefined") return null;
  188 |   return value;
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | async function requireCanPost(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  192 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
  193 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
  194 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
  195 |   ]);
  196 | }
  197 | 
  198 | function parseCommentContent(
  199 |   body: Record<string, unknown>,
  200 | ): ValidationResult<CommentContent> {
  201 |   const legacyBodyPlain = normString(body.bodyPlain);
  202 |   const bodyRichInput = "bodyRich" in body ? (body.bodyRich ?? null) : null;
  203 | 
  204 |   if (bodyRichInput === null || typeof bodyRichInput === "undefined") {
  205 |     if (!legacyBodyPlain) return validationError(400, "Missing bodyPlain.");
  206 |     if (legacyBodyPlain.length > 5000) {
  207 |       return validationError(400, "bodyPlain too long.");
  208 |     }
  209 | 
  210 |     return {
  211 |       ok: true,
  212 |       value: {
  213 |         bodyPlain: legacyBodyPlain,
  214 |         bodyRichJson: EMPTY_BODY_RICH_JSON,
  215 |       },
  216 |     };
  217 |   }
  218 | 
  219 |   const validated = validateAndSanitizeTipTapDoc(bodyRichInput);
  220 |   if (!validated.ok) return validationError(400, validated.error);
  221 | 
  222 |   let bodyRichJson = "";
  223 |   try {
  224 |     bodyRichJson = JSON.stringify(validated.doc);
  225 |   } catch {
  226 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid bodyRich.");
  227 |   }
  228 | 
  229 |   if (bodyRichJson.length > 200_000) {
  230 |     return validationError(400, "bodyRich too large.");
  231 |   }
  232 | 
  233 |   return {
  234 |     ok: true,
  235 |     value: {
  236 |       bodyPlain: validated.plain,
  237 |       bodyRichJson,
  238 |     },
  239 |   };
  240 | }
  241 | 
  242 | function parseCommentCommandDraft(
  243 |   raw: unknown,
  244 | ): ValidationResult<CommentCommandDraft> {
  245 |   const body = bodyRecord(raw);
  246 |   if (!body) return validationError(400, "Invalid JSON body.");
  247 | 
  248 |   const content = parseCommentContent(body);
  249 |   if (!content.ok) return content;
  250 | 
  251 |   const recordingId = normString(body.recordingId);
  252 |   if (!recordingId) return validationError(400, "Missing recordingId.");
  253 | 
  254 |   const lineKey = normString(body.lineKey);
  255 |   if (!lineKey) return validationError(400, "Missing lineKey.");
  256 | 
  257 |   return {
  258 |     ok: true,
  259 |     value: {
  260 |       ...content.value,
  261 |       recordingId,
  262 |       lineKey,
  263 |       groupKeyClient: normString(body.groupKey),
  264 |       parentId: normNullableId(body.parentId),
  265 |       lineTextSnapshot: normString(body.lineTextSnapshot),
  266 |       lyricsVersion: normString(body.lyricsVersion) || null,
  267 |       tMs: clampInt(body.tMs, 0, 60 * 60 * 1000),
  268 |     },
  269 |   };
  270 | }
  271 | 
  272 | async function resolveCommentCommand(
  273 |   draft: CommentCommandDraft,
  274 | ): Promise<ValidationResult<CommentCommand>> {
  275 |   const resolved = await resolveGroupKeyForAnchor({
  276 |     recordingId: draft.recordingId,
  277 |     lineKey: draft.lineKey,
  278 |   });
  279 |   const groupKey = resolved.groupKey;
  280 | 
  281 |   if (!groupKey) return validationError(400, "Could not resolve groupKey.");
  282 | 
  283 |   if (draft.groupKeyClient && draft.groupKeyClient !== groupKey) {
  284 |     return validationError(409, "Group key changed. Refresh and try again.");
  285 |   }
  286 | 
  287 |   if (!draft.bodyPlain) return validationError(400, "Missing bodyPlain.");
  288 |   if (draft.bodyPlain.length > 5000) {
  289 |     return validationError(400, "bodyPlain too long.");
  290 |   }
  291 |   if (!draft.lineTextSnapshot) {
  292 |     return validationError(400, "Missing lineTextSnapshot.");
  293 |   }
  294 |   if (draft.lineTextSnapshot.length > 2000) {
  295 |     return validationError(400, "lineTextSnapshot too long.");
  296 |   }
  297 |   if (draft.parentId && !isUuid(draft.parentId)) {
  298 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid parentId.");
  299 |   }
  300 | 
  301 |   return {
  302 |     ok: true,
  303 |     value: {
  304 |       recordingId: draft.recordingId,
  305 |       lineKey: draft.lineKey,
  306 |       groupKey,
  307 |       parentId: draft.parentId,
  308 |       bodyRichJson: draft.bodyRichJson,
  309 |       bodyPlain: draft.bodyPlain,
  310 |       lineTextSnapshot: draft.lineTextSnapshot,
  311 |       lyricsVersion: draft.lyricsVersion,
  312 |       tMs: draft.tMs,
  313 |     },
  314 |   };
  315 | }
  316 | 
  317 | async function readCommentCommand(
  318 |   req: NextRequest,
  319 | ): Promise<ValidationResult<CommentCommand>> {
  320 |   let raw: unknown;
  321 | 
  322 |   try {
  323 |     raw = await req.json();
  324 |   } catch {
  325 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid JSON body.");
  326 |   }
  327 | 
  328 |   const draft = parseCommentCommandDraft(raw);
  329 |   if (!draft.ok) return draft;
  330 | 
  331 |   return resolveCommentCommand(draft.value);
  332 | }
  333 | 
  334 | async function resolvePostingAuthority(
  335 |   req: NextRequest,
  336 |   correlationId: string,
  337 | ): Promise<PostingAuthorityResult> {
  338 |   const memberId = await requireExegesisMemberId();
  339 | 
  340 |   if (!memberId) {
  341 |     return {
  342 |       ok: false,
  343 |       response: gateError(req, {
  344 |         correlationId,
  345 |         status: 401,
  346 |         domain: "exegesis",
  347 |         code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  348 |         action: "login",
  349 |         message: "Sign in to post a comment.",
  350 |       }),
  351 |     };
  352 |   }
  353 | 
  354 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  355 |     return {
  356 |       ok: false,
  357 |       response: gateError(req, {
  358 |         correlationId,
  359 |         status: 403,
  360 |         domain: "exegesis",
  361 |         code: "PROVISIONING",
  362 |         action: "wait",
  363 |         message: "Still setting things up. Try again shortly.",
  364 |       }),
  365 |     };
  366 |   }
  367 | 
  368 |   const canPost = await requireCanPost(memberId);
  369 |   if (!canPost) {
  370 |     return {
  371 |       ok: false,
  372 |       response: gateError(req, {
  373 |         correlationId,
  374 |         status: 403,
  375 |         domain: "exegesis",
  376 |         code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  377 |         action: "subscribe",
  378 |         message: "Posting requires Patron or Partner.",
  379 |       }),
  380 |     };
  381 |   }
  382 | 
  383 |   const canonicalIdentity = await ensureMemberIdentity(memberId);
  384 | 
  385 |   return {
  386 |     ok: true,
  387 |     memberId,
  388 |     identity: canonicalIdentity,
  389 |   };
  390 | }
  391 | 
  392 | async function insertComment(params: {
  393 |   command: CommentCommand;
  394 |   memberId: string;
  395 |   anonLabel: string;
  396 | }): Promise<CommentInsertRow | null> {
  397 |   const { command, memberId, anonLabel } = params;
  398 |   const {
  399 |     recordingId,
  400 |     groupKey,
  401 |     lineKey,
  402 |     parentId,
  403 |     bodyRichJson,
  404 |     bodyPlain,
  405 |     tMs,
  406 |     lineTextSnapshot,
  407 |     lyricsVersion,
  408 |   } = command;
  409 | 
  410 |   const rootIdForRootComment = crypto.randomUUID();
  411 |   const commentIdForReply = crypto.randomUUID();
  412 |   const parentUuid = parentId;
  413 | 
  414 |   const result = await sql<CommentInsertRow>`
  415 |       with
  416 | params as (
  417 |   select
  418 |     ${recordingId}::text          as track_id,
  419 |     ${groupKey}::text             as group_key,
  420 |     ${lineKey}::text              as line_key,
  421 |     nullif(${parentUuid}::text, '')::uuid as parent_id,
  422 |       ${memberId}::uuid             as member_id,
  423 |     ${anonLabel}::text            as anon_label,
  424 |     ${bodyRichJson}::jsonb        as body_rich,
  425 |     ${bodyPlain}::text            as body_plain,
  426 |     ${tMs}::int                   as t_ms,
  427 |     ${lineTextSnapshot}::text     as line_text_snapshot,
  428 |     ${lyricsVersion}::text        as lyrics_version,
  429 |     ${rootIdForRootComment}::uuid as root_id_for_root,
  430 |     ${commentIdForReply}::uuid    as id_for_reply
  431 | ),
  432 | meta_base as (
  433 |   insert into exegesis_thread_meta (track_id, group_key)
  434 |   select p.track_id, p.group_key
  435 |   from params p
  436 |   on conflict (track_id, group_key) do nothing
  437 |   returning track_id, group_key, pinned_comment_id, locked,
  438 |             comment_count, last_activity_at, created_at, updated_at
  439 | ),
  440 | meta_existing as (
  441 |   select m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  442 |          m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  443 |   from exegesis_thread_meta m
  444 |   join params p
  445 |     on p.track_id = m.track_id and p.group_key = m.group_key
  446 |   limit 1
  447 | ),
  448 | meta_pre as (
  449 |   select * from meta_base
  450 |   union all
  451 |   select * from meta_existing
  452 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_base)
  453 | ),
  454 | ident_base as (
  455 |   insert into exegesis_identity (member_id, anon_label)
  456 |   select p.member_id, p.anon_label
  457 |   from params p
  458 |   on conflict (member_id) do nothing
  459 |   returning member_id, public_name_unlocked_at, contribution_count
  460 | ),
  461 | ident_existing as (
  462 |   select i.member_id, i.public_name_unlocked_at, i.contribution_count
  463 |   from exegesis_identity i
  464 |   join params p on p.member_id = i.member_id
  465 |   limit 1
  466 | ),
  467 | ident_pre as (
  468 |   select * from ident_base
  469 |   union all
  470 |   select * from ident_existing
  471 |   where not exists (select 1 from ident_base)
  472 | ),
  473 | parent_row as (
  474 |   select c.id, c.track_id, c.group_key, c.root_id, c.depth
  475 |   from exegesis_comment c
  476 |   join params p on c.id = p.parent_id
  477 |   limit 1
  478 | ),
  479 | parent_facts as (
  480 |   select
  481 |     p.parent_id is not null                          as has_parent,
  482 |     (select id from parent_row)                      as parent_id_found,
  483 |     (select track_id from parent_row)                as parent_track_id,
  484 |     (select group_key from parent_row)               as parent_group_key,
  485 |     (select root_id from parent_row)                 as parent_root_id,
  486 |     (select depth from parent_row)                   as parent_depth
  487 |   from params p
  488 | ),
  489 | guard_row as (
  490 |   select
  491 |     case
  492 |       when (select track_id from meta_pre) is null then 'META_MISSING'
  493 |       when (select member_id from ident_pre) is null then 'IDENT_MISSING'
  494 |       when (select locked from meta_pre) then 'LOCKED'
  495 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  496 |            and (select parent_id_found from parent_facts) is null then 'PARENT_NOT_FOUND'
  497 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  498 |            and (select parent_track_id from parent_facts) <> (select track_id from params) then 'PARENT_SCOPE'
  499 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  500 |            and (select parent_group_key from parent_facts) <> (select group_key from params) then 'PARENT_SCOPE'
  501 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  502 |            and ((select parent_depth from parent_facts) + 1) > 6 then 'DEPTH'
  503 |       else null
  504 |     end as err
  505 | ),
  506 | resolved as (
  507 |   select
  508 |     case
  509 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then (select root_id_for_root from params)
  510 |       else (select parent_root_id from parent_facts)
  511 |     end as root_id,
  512 |     case
  513 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then 0::int
  514 |       else ((select parent_depth from parent_facts) + 1)::int
  515 |     end as depth,
  516 |     case
  517 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then (select root_id_for_root from params)
  518 |       else (select id_for_reply from params)
  519 |     end as id
  520 | ),
  521 | inserted as (
  522 |   insert into exegesis_comment (
  523 |     id, track_id, group_key, line_key, parent_id, root_id, depth,
  524 |     body_rich, body_plain, t_ms, line_text_snapshot, lyrics_version,
  525 |     created_by_member_id, status
  526 |   )
  527 |   select
  528 |     r.id,
  529 |     p.track_id,
  530 |     p.group_key,
  531 |     p.line_key,
  532 |     p.parent_id,
  533 |     r.root_id,
  534 |     r.depth,
  535 |     p.body_rich,
  536 |     p.body_plain,
  537 |     p.t_ms,
  538 |     p.line_text_snapshot,
  539 |     p.lyrics_version,
  540 |     p.member_id,
  541 |     'live'
  542 |   from params p
  543 |   cross join resolved r
  544 |   cross join guard_row g
  545 |   where g.err is null
  546 |   returning
  547 |     id, track_id, group_key, line_key, parent_id, root_id, depth,
  548 |     body_rich, body_plain, t_ms, line_text_snapshot, lyrics_version,
  549 |     created_by_member_id, status::text as status,
  550 |     created_at, edited_at, edit_count, vote_count
  551 | ),
  552 | meta_upd as (
  553 |   update exegesis_thread_meta m
  554 |   set
  555 |     comment_count = m.comment_count + 1,
  556 |     last_activity_at = now(),
  557 |     updated_at = now()
  558 |   from params p
  559 |   where m.track_id = p.track_id
  560 |     and m.group_key = p.group_key
  561 |     and exists (select 1 from inserted)
  562 |   returning m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  563 |             m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  564 | ),
  565 | ident_upd as (
  566 |   update exegesis_identity i
  567 |   set
  568 |     contribution_count = i.contribution_count + 1,
  569 |     public_name_unlocked_at = case
  570 |       when i.public_name_unlocked_at is null and (i.contribution_count + 1) >= 5 then now()
  571 |       else i.public_name_unlocked_at
  572 |     end,
  573 |     updated_at = now()
  574 |   from params p
  575 |   where i.member_id = p.member_id
  576 |     and exists (select 1 from inserted)
  577 |   returning i.member_id, i.public_name_unlocked_at, i.contribution_count
  578 | ),
  579 | meta_out as (
  580 |   select * from meta_upd
  581 |   union all
  582 |   select * from meta_pre
  583 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_upd)
  584 | ),
  585 | ident_out as (
  586 |   select * from ident_upd
  587 |   union all
  588 |   select * from ident_pre
  589 |   where not exists (select 1 from ident_upd)
  590 | ),
  591 | stats as (
  592 |   select
  593 |     (select err from guard_row) as guard_err,
  594 |     (select count(*)::int from inserted) as inserted_count
  595 | )
  596 | select
  597 |   s.inserted_count,
  598 |   s.guard_err,
  599 |   i.id,
  600 |   i.track_id,
  601 |   i.group_key,
  602 |   i.line_key,
  603 |   i.parent_id,
  604 |   i.root_id,
  605 |   i.depth,
  606 |   i.body_rich,
  607 |   i.body_plain,
  608 |   i.t_ms,
  609 |   i.line_text_snapshot,
  610 |   i.lyrics_version,
  611 |   i.created_by_member_id,
  612 |   i.status,
  613 |   i.created_at,
  614 |   i.edited_at,
  615 |   i.edit_count,
  616 |   i.vote_count,
  617 |   m.track_id as meta_track_id,
  618 |   m.group_key as meta_group_key,
  619 |   m.pinned_comment_id as meta_pinned_comment_id,
  620 |   m.locked as meta_locked,
  621 |   m.comment_count as meta_comment_count,
  622 |   m.last_activity_at as meta_last_activity_at,
  623 |   m.created_at as meta_created_at,
  624 |   m.updated_at as meta_updated_at,
  625 |    u.member_id as ident_member_id,
  626 |   u.public_name_unlocked_at as ident_public_name_unlocked_at,
  627 |   u.contribution_count as ident_contribution_count
  628 | from stats s
  629 | join meta_out m on true
  630 | join ident_out u on true
  631 | left join inserted i on true
  632 | limit 1
  633 |   `;
  634 | 
  635 |   return result.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  636 | }
  637 | 
  638 | function guardFailureResponse(
  639 |   req: NextRequest,
  640 |   correlationId: string,
  641 |   guardError: string | null,
  642 | ): NextResponse {
  643 |   switch (guardError) {
  644 |     case "LOCKED":
  645 |       return gateError(req, {
  646 |         correlationId,
  647 |         status: 403,
  648 |         domain: "exegesis",
  649 |         code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  650 |         action: "wait",
  651 |         message: "This thread is locked.",
  652 |       });
  653 |     case "PARENT_NOT_FOUND":
  654 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  655 |         ok: false,
  656 |         error: "Parent not found.",
  657 |       });
  658 |     case "PARENT_SCOPE":
  659 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  660 |         ok: false,
  661 |         error: "Parent scope mismatch.",
  662 |       });
  663 |     case "DEPTH":
  664 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  665 |         ok: false,
  666 |         error: "Thread depth limit reached.",
  667 |       });
  668 |     case "META_MISSING":
  669 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  670 |         ok: false,
  671 |         error: "Thread meta missing.",
  672 |       });
  673 |     case "IDENT_MISSING":
  674 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  675 |         ok: false,
  676 |         error: "Identity missing.",
  677 |       });
  678 |     default:
  679 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  680 |         ok: false,
  681 |         error: "Insert suppressed unexpectedly.",
  682 |       });
  683 |   }
  684 | }
  685 | 
  686 | function assertInsertedRow(
  687 |   row: CommentInsertRow,
  688 | ): asserts row is InsertedCommentRow {
  689 |   if (row.inserted_count !== 1) {
  690 |     throw new Error("assertInsertedRow: inserted_count != 1");
  691 |   }
  692 | 
  693 |   if (
  694 |     !row.id ||
  695 |     !row.track_id ||
  696 |     !row.group_key ||
  697 |     !row.line_key ||
  698 |     !row.root_id ||
  699 |     typeof row.depth !== "number" ||
  700 |     row.body_plain === null ||
  701 |     row.line_text_snapshot === null ||
  702 |     !row.created_by_member_id ||
  703 |     !row.status ||
  704 |     !row.created_at ||
  705 |     typeof row.edit_count !== "number" ||
  706 |     typeof row.vote_count !== "number"
  707 |   ) {
  708 |     throw new Error("assertInsertedRow: missing required comment fields");
  709 |   }
  710 | }
  711 | 
  712 | function resolveInsertResult(
  713 |   req: NextRequest,
  714 |   correlationId: string,
  715 |   row: CommentInsertRow | null,
  716 | ): InsertResolution {
  717 |   if (!row) {
  718 |     return {
  719 |       ok: false,
  720 |       response: jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  721 |         ok: false,
  722 |         error: "No response row.",
  723 |       }),
  724 |     };
  725 |   }
  726 | 
  727 |   if ((row.inserted_count ?? 0) === 0) {
  728 |     return {
  729 |       ok: false,
  730 |       response: guardFailureResponse(req, correlationId, row.guard_err),
  731 |     };
  732 |   }
  733 | 
  734 |   if (
  735 |     !row.id ||
  736 |     !row.track_id ||
  737 |     !row.group_key ||
  738 |     !row.line_key ||
  739 |     !row.root_id
  740 |   ) {
  741 |     return {
  742 |       ok: false,
  743 |       response: jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  744 |         ok: false,
  745 |         error: "Insert returned incomplete row.",
  746 |       }),
  747 |     };
  748 |   }
  749 | 
  750 |   assertInsertedRow(row);
  751 |   return { ok: true, row };
  752 | }
  753 | 
  754 | function buildCommentDto(row: InsertedCommentRow): CommentDTO {
  755 |   return {
  756 |     id: row.id,
  757 |     recordingId: row.track_id,
  758 |     groupKey: row.group_key,
  759 |     lineKey: row.line_key,
  760 |     parentId: row.parent_id,
  761 |     rootId: row.root_id,
  762 |     depth: row.depth,
  763 |     bodyRich: row.body_rich,
  764 |     bodyPlain: row.body_plain,
  765 |     tMs: row.t_ms,
  766 |     lineTextSnapshot: row.line_text_snapshot,
  767 |     lyricsVersion: row.lyrics_version,
  768 |     createdByMemberId: row.created_by_member_id,
  769 |     status: row.status,
  770 |     createdAt: row.created_at,
  771 |     editedAt: row.edited_at,
  772 |     editCount: row.edit_count,
  773 |     voteCount: row.vote_count,
  774 |     viewerHasVoted: false,
  775 |   };
  776 | }

  783 |     locked: row.meta_locked,

  785 |     lastActivityAt: row.meta_last_activity_at,
  786 |     createdAt: row.meta_created_at,
  787 |     updatedAt: row.meta_updated_at,
  788 |   };
  789 | }

  791 | function buildIdentityMap(
  792 |   row: InsertedCommentRow,
  793 |   identity: PostingIdentity,
  794 | ): Record<string, IdentityDTO> {
  795 |   return {

  799 |       publicName: identity.publicName,

  801 |       contributionCount: row.ident_contribution_count,
  802 |       isAdmin: identity.isAdmin,
  803 |     },
  804 |   };
  805 | }
  806 | 
  807 | async function runPostCommitBadgeEffects(params: {
  808 |   memberId: string;
  809 |   recordingId: string;
  810 |   publicNameUnlockedAt: string | null;
  811 |   correlationId: string;
  812 | }): Promise<NewlyAwardedBadge[]> {
  813 |   let badges: NewlyAwardedBadge[];
  814 | 
  815 |   try {
  816 |     badges = await runAutoBadgeAwardsForMember({
  817 |       memberId: params.memberId,
  818 |       trigger:
  819 |         params.publicNameUnlockedAt !== null
  820 |           ? "public_name_unlocked"
  821 |           : "exegesis_contribution_created",
  822 |       recordingId: params.recordingId,
  823 |       grantedBy: "system",
  824 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  825 |     });
  826 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  827 |     console.error("[exegesis/comment] post-commit badge award failed", error);
  828 |     return [];
  829 |   }
  830 | 
  831 |   if (badges.length === 0) return badges;
  832 | 
  833 |   try {
  834 |     await markOverlayAnnouncedForAwardedBadges({
  835 |       memberId: params.memberId,
  836 |       badges,
  837 |     });
  838 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  839 |     console.error(
  840 |       "[exegesis/comment] post-commit badge announcement failed",
  841 |       error,
  842 |     );
  843 |   }
  844 | 
  845 |   return badges;
  846 | }
  847 | 
  848 | function describeUnexpectedError(error: unknown): string {
  849 |   const details =
  850 |     typeof error === "object" && error !== null
  851 |       ? (error as {
  852 |           code?: string;
  853 |           message?: string;
  854 |           detail?: string;
  855 |           hint?: string;
  856 |         })
  857 |       : null;
  858 | 
  859 |   let message = "";
  860 |   if (typeof details?.message === "string" && details.message.trim()) {
  861 |     message = details.message.trim();
  862 |   } else if (error instanceof Error && error.message.trim()) {
  863 |     message = error.message.trim();
  864 |   }
  865 | 
  866 |   const extras = [details?.code, details?.detail, details?.hint].filter(
  867 |     (value): value is string =>
  868 |       typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0,
  869 |   );
  870 | 
  871 |   return `${message || "Unknown error."}${
  872 |     extras.length > 0 ? ` (${extras.join(" · ")})` : ""
  873 |   }`;
  874 | }
  875 | 
  876 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  877 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  878 | 
  879 |   const command = await readCommentCommand(req);
  880 |   if (!command.ok) {
  881 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, command.status, {
  882 |       ok: false,
  883 |       error: command.error,
  884 |     });
  885 |   }
  886 | 
  887 |   const authority = await resolvePostingAuthority(req, correlationId);
  888 |   if (!authority.ok) return authority.response;
  889 | 
  890 |   try {
  891 |     const insertRow = await insertComment({
  892 |       command: command.value,
  893 |       memberId: authority.memberId,
  894 |       anonLabel: authority.identity.anonLabel,
  895 |     });
  896 | 
  897 |     const insert = resolveInsertResult(req, correlationId, insertRow);
  898 |     if (!insert.ok) return insert.response;
  899 | 
  900 |     const { row } = insert;
  901 |     const identities = buildIdentityMap(row, authority.identity);
  902 |     const newlyAwardedBadges = await runPostCommitBadgeEffects({
  903 |       memberId: authority.memberId,
  904 |       recordingId: command.value.recordingId,
  905 |       publicNameUnlockedAt: row.ident_public_name_unlocked_at,
  906 |       correlationId,
  907 |     });
  908 | 
  909 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  910 |       {
  911 |         ok: true,
  912 |         recordingId: command.value.recordingId,
  913 |         groupKey: command.value.groupKey,
  914 |         comment: buildCommentDto(row),
  915 |         meta: buildThreadMetaDto(row),
  916 |         identities,
  917 |         newlyAwardedBadges,
  918 |       },
  919 |       { correlationId },
  920 |     );
  921 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  922 |     console.error("[exegesis/comment] POST failed", error);
  923 | 
  924 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  925 |       ok: false,
  926 |       error: describeUnexpectedError(error),
  927 |     });
  928 |   }
  929 | }

   20 | type ApiOk = { ok: true; reportId: string };
   21 | type ApiErr = {
   22 |   ok: false;
   23 |   error: string;

   27 | 
   28 | function jsonErr(correlationId: string, status: number, body: ApiErr) {
   29 |   return withCorrelationId(NextResponse.json(body, { status }), correlationId);
   30 | }
   31 | 
   32 | function norm(v: unknown): string {
   33 |   return typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "";
   34 | }
   35 | 
   36 | function isUuid(v: string): boolean {
   37 |   return /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
   38 |     v,
   39 |   );
   40 | }

   42 | async function requireMemberId(): Promise<string | null> {
   43 |   const { userId } = await auth();
   44 |   if (!userId) return null;
   45 | 
   46 |   const r = await sql<{ id: string }>`
   47 |     select id
   48 |     from members
   49 |     where clerk_user_id = ${userId}
   50 |     limit 1
   51 |   `;
   52 |   const memberId = r.rows?.[0]?.id ?? "";
   53 |   return memberId || null;
   54 | }
   55 | 
   56 | async function requireCanReport(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   57 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
   58 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_FRIEND,
   59 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
   60 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
   61 |   ]);
   62 | }
   63 | 
   64 | const CATEGORIES = new Set([
   65 |   "spam",
   66 |   "harassment",
   67 |   "hate",
   68 |   "sexual",
   69 |   "self_harm",
   70 |   "violence",
   71 |   "misinfo",
   72 |   "copyright",
   73 |   "other",
   74 | ]);
   75 | 
   76 | function validateCategory(raw: string): string | null {
   77 |   const c = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
   78 |   return CATEGORIES.has(c) ? c : null;
   79 | }
   80 | 
   81 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   82 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   83 | 
   84 |   let raw: unknown;
   85 |   try {
   86 |     raw = await req.json();
   87 |   } catch {
   88 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   89 |       ok: false,
   90 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   91 |     });
   92 |   }
   93 | 
   94 |   const b =
   95 |     typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null
   96 |       ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>)
   97 |       : null;
   98 |   if (!b) {
   99 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  100 |       ok: false,
  101 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
  102 |     });
  103 |   }
  104 | 
  105 |   const commentId = norm(b.commentId);
  106 |   const categoryRaw = norm(b.category);
  107 |   const reason = norm(b.reason);
  108 | 
  109 |   if (!commentId) {
  110 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  111 |       ok: false,
  112 |       error: "Missing commentId.",
  113 |     });
  114 |   }
  115 |   if (!isUuid(commentId)) {
  116 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  117 |       ok: false,
  118 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
  119 |     });
  120 |   }
  121 | 
  122 |   const category = validateCategory(categoryRaw);
  123 |   if (!category) {
  124 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  125 |       ok: false,
  126 |       error: "Invalid category.",
  127 |     });
  128 |   }
  129 | 
  130 |   if (reason.length < 20) {
  131 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  132 |       ok: false,
  133 |       error: "Reason must be at least 20 characters.",
  134 |     });
  135 |   }
  136 |   if (reason.length > 300) {
  137 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  138 |       ok: false,
  139 |       error: "Reason must be 300 characters or less.",
  140 |     });
  141 |   }
  142 | 
  143 |   const memberId = await requireMemberId();
  144 |   if (!memberId) {
  145 |     return gateError(req, {
  146 |       correlationId,
  147 |       status: 401,
  148 |       domain: "exegesis",
  149 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  150 |       action: "login",
  151 |       message: "Sign in to report a comment.",
  152 |     });
  153 |   }
  154 | 
  155 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  156 |     return gateError(req, {
  157 |       correlationId,
  158 |       status: 403,
  159 |       domain: "exegesis",
  160 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  161 |       action: "wait",
  162 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  163 |     });
  164 |   }
  165 | 
  166 |   const canReport = await requireCanReport(memberId);
  167 |   if (!canReport) {
  168 |     return gateError(req, {
  169 |       correlationId,
  170 |       status: 403,
  171 |       domain: "exegesis",
  172 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  173 |       action: "subscribe",
  174 |       message: "Reporting requires Friend tier or higher.",
  175 |     });
  176 |   }
  177 | 
  178 |   try {
  179 |     const ins = await sql<{ id: string; comment_status: string | null }>`
  180 |       with c as (
  181 |         select id, status::text as status
  182 |         from exegesis_comment
  183 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  184 |         limit 1
  185 |       ),
  186 |       inserted as (
  187 |         insert into exegesis_report (comment_id, reporter_member_id, category, reason)
  188 |         select
  189 |           c.id,
  190 |           ${memberId}::uuid,
  191 |           ${category},
  192 |           ${reason}
  193 |         from c
  194 |         where c.id is not null
  195 |           and c.status <> 'deleted'
  196 |           and not exists (
  197 |             select 1
  198 |             from exegesis_report r
  199 |             where r.comment_id = c.id
  200 |               and r.reporter_member_id = ${memberId}::uuid
  201 |           )
  202 |         returning id
  203 |       )
  204 |       select
  205 |         (select id from inserted limit 1) as id,
  206 |         (select status from c limit 1) as comment_status
  207 |     `;
  208 | 
  209 |     const reportId = ins.rows?.[0]?.id ?? "";
  210 |     const commentStatus = (ins.rows?.[0]?.comment_status ?? null) as
  211 |       | "live"
  212 |       | "hidden"
  213 |       | "deleted"
  214 |       | null;
  215 | 
  216 |     if (!commentStatus) {
  217 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  218 |         ok: false,
  219 |         error: "Comment not found.",
  220 |       });
  221 |     }
  222 |     if (commentStatus === "deleted") {
  223 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  224 |         ok: false,
  225 |         error: "Cannot report a deleted comment.",
  226 |       });
  227 |     }
  228 | 
  229 |     if (!reportId) {
  230 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 409, {
  231 |         ok: false,
  232 |         code: "ALREADY_REPORTED",
  233 |         error: "You’ve already reported this comment.",
  234 |       });
  235 |     }
  236 | 
  237 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>({ ok: true, reportId }, { correlationId });
  238 |   } catch (e: unknown) {
  239 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  240 |       ok: false,
  241 |       error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error.",
  242 |     });
  243 |   }
  244 | }

  170 |   updated_at: string;
  171 | };
  172 | 
  173 | async function capsForMember(memberId: string): Promise<{
  174 |   canVote: boolean;
  175 |   canReport: boolean;
  176 |   canPost: boolean;
  177 | }> {
  178 |   const canVote = await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
  179 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_FRIEND,
  180 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
  181 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
  182 |   ]);
  183 |   const canReport = canVote;
  184 |   const canPost = await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
  185 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
  186 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
  187 |   ]);
  188 |   return { canVote, canReport, canPost };
  189 | }

  191 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  192 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  193 |   const url = new URL(req.url);
  194 | 
  195 |   const recordingId = norm(url.searchParams.get("recordingId"));
  196 |   const sortParam = norm(url.searchParams.get("sort")) || "top";
  197 |   const sort: ThreadSort = isSort(sortParam) ? sortParam : "top";
  198 | 
  199 |   if (!recordingId) {
  200 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  201 |       ok: false,
  202 |       error: "Missing recordingId.",
  203 |     });
  204 |   }
  205 |   if (recordingId.length > 200) {
  206 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  207 |       ok: false,
  208 |       error: "Invalid recordingId.",
  209 |     });
  210 |   }
  211 | 
  212 |   const rawGroupKey = norm(url.searchParams.get("groupKey"));
  213 |   const lineKey = norm(url.searchParams.get("lineKey"));
  214 | 
  215 |   let groupKey = "";
  216 | 
  217 |   if (lineKey) {
  218 |     const resolved = await resolveGroupKeyForAnchor({ recordingId, lineKey });
  219 |     groupKey = resolved.groupKey;
  220 | 
  221 |     if (rawGroupKey && norm(rawGroupKey) !== groupKey) {
  222 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 409, {
  223 |         ok: false,
  224 |         error: "Group key changed. Refresh and try again.",
  225 |       });
  226 |     }
  227 |   } else {
  228 |     if (!rawGroupKey) {
  229 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  230 |         ok: false,
  231 |         error: "Missing lineKey (or groupKey).",
  232 |       });
  233 |     }
  234 | 
  235 |     const g = norm(rawGroupKey);
  236 | 
  237 |     if (isGroupKeyV1(g)) {
  238 |       groupKey = g;
  239 |     } else {
  240 |       const ok = await isKnownCanonicalGroupKey({ recordingId, groupKey: g });
  241 |       if (!ok) {
  242 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  243 |           ok: false,
  244 |           error: "Unknown groupKey.",
  245 |         });
  246 |       }
  247 |       groupKey = g;
  248 |     }
  249 |   }
  250 | 
  251 |   if (!groupKey) {
  252 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  253 |       ok: false,
  254 |       error: "Invalid group key.",
  255 |     });
  256 |   }
  257 | 
  258 |   const bootstrapRes = jsonOk({ ok: true }, { correlationId });
  259 |   const ensured = ensureAnonId(req, bootstrapRes);
  260 |   const { anonId } = ensured;
  261 | 
  262 |   const viewer = await getViewer(req, anonId);
  263 | 
  264 |   const viewerMemberId =
  265 |     viewer.kind === "member" && isUuid(viewer.memberId)
  266 |       ? viewer.memberId
  267 |       : null;
  268 | 
  269 |   if (viewerMemberId) {
  270 |     await ensureMemberIdentity(viewerMemberId);
  271 |   }
  272 | 
  273 |   if (viewer.kind === "anon") {
  274 |     const LIMIT = 8;
  275 |     const sessionId = anonId;
  276 | 
  277 |     await sql`
  278 |       insert into anon_exegesis_sessions (id, anon_id)
  279 |       values (${sessionId}, ${anonId})
  280 |       on conflict (id) do nothing
  281 |     `;
  282 | 
  283 |     const gateRes = await sql<{
  284 |       already: boolean;
  285 |       allowed: boolean;
  286 |       n_before: number;
  287 |       n_after: number;
  288 |     }>`
  289 |       with
  290 |       already as (
  291 |         select exists(
  292 |           select 1
  293 |           from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  294 |           where session_id = ${sessionId}
  295 |             and track_id = ${recordingId}
  296 |             and group_key = ${groupKey}
  297 |         ) as already
  298 |       ),
  299 |       cnt_before as (
  300 |         select count(*)::int as n_before
  301 |         from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  302 |         where session_id = ${sessionId}
  303 |       ),
  304 |       attempt as (
  305 |         insert into anon_exegesis_thread_opens (session_id, track_id, group_key)
  306 |         select ${sessionId}, ${recordingId}, ${groupKey}
  307 |         where (select already from already) = false
  308 |           and (select n_before from cnt_before) < ${LIMIT}
  309 |         on conflict (session_id, track_id, group_key) do nothing
  310 |         returning 1
  311 |       ),
  312 |       cnt_after as (
  313 |         select count(*)::int as n_after
  314 |         from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  315 |         where session_id = ${sessionId}
  316 |       )
  317 |       select
  318 |         (select already from already) as already,
  319 |         ((select already from already) = true or exists(select 1 from attempt)) as allowed,
  320 |         (select n_before from cnt_before) as n_before,
  321 |         (select n_after from cnt_after) as n_after
  322 |     `;
  323 | 
  324 |     const row = gateRes.rows?.[0];
  325 |     const allowed = Boolean(row?.allowed);
  326 | 
  327 |     if (!allowed) {
  328 |       const message = "Sign in to keep reading.";
  329 | 
  330 |       return gateError(req, {
  331 |         correlationId,
  332 |         status: 403,
  333 |         domain: "exegesis",
  334 |         code: "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED",
  335 |         action: "login",
  336 |         message,
  337 |         onResponse: (res) => {
  338 |           for (const [k, v] of bootstrapRes.headers.entries()) {
  339 |             if (k.toLowerCase() === "set-cookie") res.headers.append(k, v);
  340 |           }
  341 |         },
  342 |       });
  343 |     }
  344 |   }
  345 | 
  346 |   const metaRes = await sql<DbThreadMetaRow>`
  347 |     select track_id, group_key, pinned_comment_id, locked, comment_count, last_activity_at, created_at, updated_at
  348 |     from exegesis_thread_meta
  349 |     where track_id = ${recordingId}
  350 |       and group_key = ${groupKey}
  351 |     limit 1
  352 |   `;
  353 |   const metaRow = metaRes.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  354 | 
  355 |   const commentsRes = await sql<DbCommentRow>`
  356 |     with base as (
  357 |       select
  358 |         c.id,
  359 |         c.track_id,
  360 |         c.group_key,
  361 |         c.line_key,
  362 |         c.parent_id,
  363 |         c.root_id,
  364 |         c.depth,
  365 |         c.body_rich,
  366 |         c.body_plain,
  367 |         c.t_ms,
  368 |         c.line_text_snapshot,
  369 |         c.lyrics_version,
  370 |         c.created_by_member_id,
  371 |         c.status::text as status,
  372 |         c.created_at,
  373 |         c.edited_at,
  374 |         c.edit_count,
  375 |         c.vote_count
  376 |       from exegesis_comment c
  377 |       where c.track_id = ${recordingId}
  378 |         and c.group_key = ${groupKey}
  379 |         and c.status <> 'deleted'
  380 |     ),
  381 |     voted as (
  382 |       select
  383 |         b.*,
  384 |         case
  385 |           when ${viewerMemberId}::uuid is null then false
  386 |           else exists (
  387 |             select 1
  388 |             from exegesis_vote v
  389 |             where v.member_id = ${viewerMemberId}::uuid
  390 |               and v.comment_id = b.id
  391 |           )
  392 |         end as viewer_has_voted
  393 |       from base b
  394 |     )
  395 |     select *
  396 |     from voted
  397 |     order by
  398 |       case when ${sort} = 'top' then (case when parent_id is null then vote_count else 0 end) end desc nulls last,
  399 |       case when ${sort} = 'recent' then (case when parent_id is null then created_at else null end) end desc nulls last,
  400 |       root_id asc,
  401 |       created_at asc
  402 |   `;
  403 | 
  404 |   const rows = commentsRes.rows ?? [];
  405 |   const byRoot = new Map<string, CommentDTO[]>();
  406 |   const authorIds = new Set<string>();
  407 | 
  408 |   for (const r of rows) {
  409 |     authorIds.add(r.created_by_member_id);
  410 | 
  411 |     const dto: CommentDTO = {
  412 |       id: r.id,
  413 |       recordingId: r.track_id,
  414 |       groupKey: r.group_key,
  415 |       lineKey: r.line_key,
  416 |       parentId: r.parent_id,
  417 |       rootId: r.root_id,
  418 |       depth: r.depth,
  419 |       bodyRich: r.body_rich,
  420 |       bodyPlain: r.body_plain,
  421 |       tMs: r.t_ms,
  422 |       lineTextSnapshot: r.line_text_snapshot,
  423 |       lyricsVersion: r.lyrics_version,
  424 |       createdByMemberId: r.created_by_member_id,
  425 |       status: r.status,
  426 |       createdAt: r.created_at,
  427 |       editedAt: r.edited_at,
  428 |       editCount: r.edit_count,
  429 |       voteCount: r.vote_count,
  430 |       viewerHasVoted: r.viewer_has_voted,
  431 |     };
  432 | 
  433 |     const arr = byRoot.get(dto.rootId) ?? [];
  434 |     arr.push(dto);
  435 |     byRoot.set(dto.rootId, arr);
  436 |   }
  437 | 
  438 |   const identities: Record<string, IdentityDTO> = {};
  439 |   const wantIds = new Set<string>();
  440 | 
  441 |   for (const id of Array.from(authorIds)) {
  442 |     const s = (id ?? "").trim();
  443 |     if (isUuid(s)) wantIds.add(s);
  444 |   }
  445 |   if (viewer.kind === "member") {
  446 |     const me = (viewer.memberId ?? "").trim();
  447 |     if (isUuid(me)) wantIds.add(me);
  448 |   }
  449 | 
  450 |   const uuids = Array.from(wantIds);
  451 | 
  452 |   if (uuids.length > 0) {
  453 |     const built = await buildExegesisIdentityDtoMap(uuids);
  454 |     for (const [memberId, identity] of Object.entries(built)) {
  455 |       identities[memberId] = identity;
  456 |     }
  457 |   }
  458 | 
  459 |   const roots = Array.from(byRoot.entries()).map(([rootId, comments]) => ({
  460 |     rootId,
  461 |     comments,
  462 |   }));
  463 | 
  464 |   const meta: ThreadMetaDTO | null = metaRow
  465 |     ? {
  466 |         recordingId: metaRow.track_id,
  467 |         groupKey: metaRow.group_key,
  468 |         pinnedCommentId: metaRow.pinned_comment_id,
  469 |         locked: metaRow.locked,
  470 |         commentCount: metaRow.comment_count,
  471 |         lastActivityAt: metaRow.last_activity_at,
  472 |         createdAt: metaRow.created_at,
  473 |         updatedAt: metaRow.updated_at,
  474 |       }
  475 |     : null;
  476 | 
  477 |   let viewerDto: ViewerDTO;
  478 | 
  479 |   if (!viewerMemberId) {
  480 |     viewerDto = { kind: "anon" };
  481 |   } else {
  482 |     const cap0 = await capsForMember(viewerMemberId);
  483 |     const locked = Boolean(metaRow?.locked);
  484 |     const me = identities[viewerMemberId];
  485 |     const canClaimName =
  486 |       !!me && !me.publicName && me.publicNameUnlockedAt != null;
  487 | 
  488 |     viewerDto = {
  489 |       kind: "member",
  490 |       memberId: viewerMemberId,
  491 |       cap: {
  492 |         ...cap0,
  493 |         canPost: locked ? false : cap0.canPost,
  494 |         canClaimName,
  495 |       },
  496 |     };
  497 |   }
  498 | 
  499 |   const okRes = jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  500 |     {
  501 |       ok: true,
  502 |       recordingId,
  503 |       groupKey,
  504 |       sort,
  505 |       meta,
  506 |       roots,
  507 |       identities,
  508 |       viewer: viewerDto,
  509 |     },
  510 |     { correlationId },
  511 |   );
  512 | 
  513 |   for (const [k, v] of bootstrapRes.headers.entries()) {
  514 |     if (k.toLowerCase() === "set-cookie") okRes.headers.append(k, v);
  515 |   }
  516 | 
  517 |   return okRes;
  518 | }

   35 | function jsonErr(correlationId: string, status: number, body: ApiErr) {
   36 |   return withCorrelationId(NextResponse.json(body, { status }), correlationId);

   39 | function norm(s: unknown): string {
   40 |   return typeof s === "string" ? s.trim() : "";

   43 | function isUuid(v: string): boolean {
   44 |   return /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
   45 |     v,
   46 |   );

   49 | async function requireMemberId(): Promise<string | null> {

   54 |     select id
   55 |     from members
   56 |     where clerk_user_id = ${userId}
   57 |     limit 1
   58 |   `;
   59 |   const memberId = r.rows?.[0]?.id ?? "";

   71 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   72 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   73 | 
   74 |   let raw: unknown;
   75 |   try {
   76 |     raw = await req.json();
   77 |   } catch {
   78 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   79 |       ok: false,
   80 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   81 |     });
   82 |   }
   83 | 
   84 |   const b =
   85 |     typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null
   86 |       ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>)
   87 |       : null;
   88 |   if (!b) {
   89 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   90 |       ok: false,
   91 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   92 |     });
   93 |   }
   94 | 
   95 |   const commentId = norm(b.commentId);
   96 |   if (!commentId) {
   97 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   98 |       ok: false,
   99 |       error: "Missing commentId.",
  100 |     });
  101 |   }
  102 |   if (!isUuid(commentId)) {
  103 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  104 |       ok: false,
  105 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
  106 |     });
  107 |   }
  108 | 
  109 |   const memberId = await requireMemberId();
  110 |   if (!memberId) {
  111 |     return gateError(req, {
  112 |       correlationId,
  113 |       status: 401,
  114 |       domain: "exegesis",
  115 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  116 |       action: "login",
  117 |       message: "Sign in to vote.",
  118 |     });
  119 |   }
  120 | 
  121 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  122 |     return gateError(req, {
  123 |       correlationId,
  124 |       status: 403,
  125 |       domain: "exegesis",
  126 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  127 |       action: "wait",
  128 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  129 |     });
  130 |   }
  131 | 
  132 |   const canVote = await requireCanVote(memberId);
  133 |   if (!canVote) {
  134 |     return gateError(req, {
  135 |       correlationId,
  136 |       status: 403,
  137 |       domain: "exegesis",
  138 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  139 |       action: "subscribe",
  140 |       message: "Voting requires Friend tier or higher.",
  141 |     });
  142 |   }
  143 | 
  144 |   try {
  145 |     const r = await sql<{
  146 |       ok: boolean;
  147 |       viewer_has_voted: boolean;
  148 |       vote_count: number;
  149 |       err: string | null;
  150 |       author_member_id: string | null;
  151 |     }>`
  152 |       with
  153 |       c as (
  154 |         select
  155 |           id,
  156 |           status::text as status,
  157 |           track_id,
  158 |           group_key,
  159 |           vote_count,
  160 |           created_by_member_id
  161 |         from exegesis_comment
  162 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  163 |         limit 1
  164 |       ),
  165 |       m as (
  166 |         select locked
  167 |         from exegesis_thread_meta
  168 |         where track_id = (select track_id from c)
  169 |           and group_key = (select group_key from c)
  170 |         limit 1
  171 |       ),
  172 |       guard as (
  173 |         select
  174 |           case
  175 |             when (select id from c) is null then 'NOT_FOUND'
  176 |             when (select status from c) = 'deleted' then 'DELETED'
  177 |             when (select status from c) = 'hidden' then 'HIDDEN'
  178 |             when coalesce((select locked from m), false) = true then 'LOCKED'
  179 |             else null
  180 |           end as err
  181 |       ),
  182 |       del as (
  183 |         delete from exegesis_vote
  184 |         where member_id = ${memberId}::uuid
  185 |           and comment_id = ${commentId}::uuid
  186 |           and (select err from guard) is null
  187 |         returning 1 as deleted
  188 |       ),
  189 |       ins as (
  190 |         insert into exegesis_vote (member_id, comment_id)
  191 |         select ${memberId}::uuid, ${commentId}::uuid
  192 |         where (select err from guard) is null
  193 |           and not exists (select 1 from del)
  194 |         on conflict (member_id, comment_id) do nothing
  195 |         returning 1 as inserted
  196 |       ),
  197 |       upd as (
  198 |         update exegesis_comment
  199 |         set vote_count = greatest(
  200 |           vote_count + (case when exists (select 1 from ins) then 1 else 0 end)
  201 |                      - (case when exists (select 1 from del) then 1 else 0 end),
  202 |           0
  203 |         )
  204 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  205 |           and (select err from guard) is null
  206 |         returning vote_count
  207 |       )
  208 |        select
  209 |         (select err from guard) is null as ok,
  210 |         case
  211 |           when (select err from guard) is not null then false
  212 |           when exists (select 1 from ins) then true
  213 |           else false
  214 |         end as viewer_has_voted,
  215 |         coalesce((select vote_count from upd), (select vote_count from c), 0)::int as vote_count,
  216 |         (select err from guard) as err,
  217 |         (select created_by_member_id::text from c) as author_member_id
  218 |     `;
  219 | 
  220 |     const row = r.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  221 |     if (!row) {
  222 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  223 |         ok: false,
  224 |         error: "Vote failed.",
  225 |       });
  226 |     }
  227 | 
  228 |     if (!row.ok) {
  229 |       if (row.err === "NOT_FOUND") {
  230 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  231 |           ok: false,
  232 |           error: "Comment not found.",
  233 |         });
  234 |       }
  235 |       if (row.err === "DELETED") {
  236 |         return gateError(req, {
  237 |           correlationId,
  238 |           status: 400,
  239 |           domain: "exegesis",
  240 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  241 |           action: "wait",
  242 |           message: "Cannot vote on deleted comment.",
  243 |         });
  244 |       }
  245 |       if (row.err === "HIDDEN") {
  246 |         return gateError(req, {
  247 |           correlationId,
  248 |           status: 403,
  249 |           domain: "exegesis",
  250 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  251 |           action: "wait",
  252 |           message: "Cannot vote on hidden comment.",
  253 |         });
  254 |       }
  255 |       if (row.err === "LOCKED") {
  256 |         return gateError(req, {
  257 |           correlationId,
  258 |           status: 403,
  259 |           domain: "exegesis",
  260 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  261 |           action: "wait",
  262 |           message: "Thread is locked.",
  263 |         });
  264 |       }
  265 | 
  266 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  267 |         ok: false,
  268 |         error: "Cannot vote on this comment.",
  269 |       });
  270 |     }
  271 | 
  272 |     const newlyAwardedBadges =
  273 |       row.author_member_id && isUuid(row.author_member_id)
  274 |         ? await runAutoBadgeAwardsForMember({
  275 |             memberId: row.author_member_id,
  276 |             trigger: "exegesis_vote_updated",
  277 |             grantedBy: "system",
  278 |             correlationId,
  279 |           })
  280 |         : [];
  281 | 
  282 |     if (row.author_member_id && isUuid(row.author_member_id)) {
  283 |       await markOverlayAnnouncedForAwardedBadges({
  284 |         memberId: row.author_member_id,
  285 |         badges: newlyAwardedBadges,
  286 |       });
  287 |     }
  288 | 
  289 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  290 |       {
  291 |         ok: true,
  292 |         commentId,
  293 |         viewerHasVoted: row.viewer_has_voted,
  294 |         voteCount: Number(row.vote_count ?? 0),
  295 |         newlyAwardedBadges,
  296 |       },
  297 |       { correlationId },
  298 |     );
  299 |   } catch (e: unknown) {
  300 |     const msg =
  301 |       e instanceof Error
  302 |         ? norm(e.message)
  303 |         : norm(typeof e === "string" ? e : "");
  304 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  305 |       ok: false,
  306 |       error: msg || "Unknown error.",
  307 |     });
  308 |   }
  309 | }

   19 |   | "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED"
   20 |   | "TIER_REQUIRED"

   22 |   | "PROVISIONING"

   29 | /**
   30 |  * Temporary compatibility codes still emitted by legacy endpoints.
   31 |  * These MUST be normalized at boundaries (AudioEngine, adapters, server responses).
   32 |  */
   33 | export type LegacyGateCode = "ANON_CAP_REACHED" | "CAP_REACHED";
   34 | 
   35 | /**
   36 |  * “Raw” is what the outside world may hand us while migration is in progress.
   37 |  * Internal gate policy should use canonical GateCode where possible.
   38 |  */
   39 | export type GateCodeRaw = GateCode | LegacyGateCode;
   40 | 
   41 | export type GateReason = {
   42 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
   43 |   action: GateAction;
   44 |   message: string;
   45 |   correlationId?: string | null;
   46 |   domain: GateDomain;
   47 | };
   48 | 
   49 | /**
   50 |  * Normalize any raw/legacy string into a known code (canonical or legacy).
   51 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   52 |  */
   53 | export function normalizeGateCodeRaw(
   54 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   55 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   56 |   const c = (raw ?? "").trim();
   57 |   if (!c) return null;
   58 | 
   59 |   switch (c) {
   60 |     // canonical
   61 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   62 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   63 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
   64 |     case "EMBARGO":
   65 |     case "PROVISIONING":
   66 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
   67 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   68 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   69 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   70 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
   71 |       return c;
   72 | 
   73 |     // legacy
   74 |     case "ANON_CAP_REACHED":
   75 |     case "CAP_REACHED":
   76 |       return c;
   77 | 
   78 |     default:
   79 |       return null;
   80 |   }
   81 | }
   82 | 
   83 | /**
   84 |  * Helper for adapters that only have “raw string” codes.
   85 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   86 |  */
   87 | export function parseGateCodeRaw(
   88 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   89 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   90 |   return normalizeGateCodeRaw(raw);
   91 | }
   92 | 
   93 | /**
   94 |  * One-way canonicalization helper for legacy cap codes.
   95 |  * Use at boundaries when you KNOW the domain context (e.g. playback vs receipts).
   96 |  */
   97 | export function canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(
   98 |   raw: GateCodeRaw,
   99 |   domain: GateDomain,
  100 | ): GateCodeRaw {
  101 |   if (raw !== "ANON_CAP_REACHED" && raw !== "CAP_REACHED") return raw;
  102 | 
  103 |   switch (domain) {
  104 |     case "playback":
  105 |       return "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED";
  106 |     case "journal":
  107 |       return "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  108 |     case "exegesis":
  109 |       return "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  110 |     default:
  111 |       // No silent semantic drift: if a legacy cap arrives without a known mapping,
  112 |       // keep it legacy and let the adapter/engine handle it explicitly.
  113 |       return raw;
  114 |   }
  115 | }
  116 | 
  117 | /**
  118 |  * Standard server->client blocked payload shape (future-proof).
  119 |  * (Not yet enforced repo-wide; introduced now so endpoints can converge.)
  120 |  */
  121 | export type GatePayload = {
  122 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
  123 |   action: GateAction;
  124 |   domain: GateDomain;
  125 |   message: string;
  126 |   correlationId?: string | null;
  127 | 
  128 |   /**
  129 |    * Optional extra detail for logs / UX copy variants.
  130 |    * Keep as string so the server doesn't leak UI policy.
  131 |    */
  132 |   reason?: string;
  133 | };

  107 | export type TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  108 |   ok: true;
  109 |   recordingId: string;
  110 |   offsetMs: number;
  111 |   cues: LyricCue[];
  112 | 
  113 |   // present in API today, but optional for consumers
  114 |   version?: string;
  115 |   geniusUrl?: string | null;
  116 |   groupMap?: LyricGroupMap;

  119 |   trackTitle?: string | null;

  122 |   albumTitle?: string | null;
  123 |   albumSlug?: string | null;
  124 |   albumCatalogueId?: string | null;
  125 | };
  126 | 
  127 | export function normalizeLyricCues(input: unknown): LyricCue[] {
  128 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  129 |   const out: LyricCue[] = [];
  130 | 
  131 |   for (const c of input) {
  132 |     if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
  133 | 
  134 |     const lineKey = (c as { lineKey?: unknown }).lineKey;
  135 |     const tMs = (c as { tMs?: unknown }).tMs;
  136 |     const text = (c as { text?: unknown }).text;
  137 |     const endMs = (c as { endMs?: unknown }).endMs;
  138 |     const canonicalGroupKey = (c as { canonicalGroupKey?: unknown })
  139 |       .canonicalGroupKey;
  140 | 
  141 |     if (typeof lineKey !== "string" || lineKey.trim().length === 0) continue;
  142 |     if (typeof tMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(tMs) || tMs < 0) continue;
  143 |     if (typeof text !== "string" || text.trim().length === 0) continue;
  144 | 
  145 |     const cue: LyricCue = {
  146 |       lineKey: lineKey.trim(),
  147 |       tMs: Math.floor(tMs),
  148 |       text: text.trim(),
  149 |     };
  150 | 
  151 |     if (typeof endMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(endMs) && endMs >= 0) {
  152 |       cue.endMs = Math.floor(endMs);
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     // optional enrichment
  156 |     if (typeof canonicalGroupKey === "string" && canonicalGroupKey.trim()) {
  157 |       cue.canonicalGroupKey = canonicalGroupKey.trim();
  158 |     }
  159 | 
  160 |     out.push(cue);
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   out.sort((a, b) => a.tMs - b.tMs);
  164 |   return out;
  165 | }
  166 | 
  167 | export type SanityLyricCue = {
  168 |   _key?: string;
  169 |   tMs?: number;
  170 |   text?: string;
  171 |   endMs?: number;
  172 | };
  173 | 
  174 | // Adapts Sanity cue shape into canonical cue normalization.
  175 | // (Sanity uses `_key` for the line key.)
  176 | export function normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity(
  177 |   input: SanityLyricCue[] | undefined | null,
  178 | ): LyricCue[] {
  179 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  180 |   return normalizeLyricCues(
  181 |     input.map((c) => ({
  182 |       lineKey: c?._key,
  183 |       tMs: c?.tMs,
  184 |       text: c?.text,
  185 |       endMs: c?.endMs,
  186 |       // canonicalGroupKey not present in Sanity lyrics docs
  187 |     })),
  188 |   );
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | export function parseTrackLyricsApiOk(raw: unknown): TrackLyricsApiOk | null {
  192 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  193 |   const o = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  194 |   if (o.ok !== true) return null;
  195 | 
  196 |   if (typeof o.recordingId !== "string" || !o.recordingId.trim()) return null;
  197 |   if (typeof o.offsetMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(o.offsetMs))
  198 |     return null;
  199 | 
  200 |   const cues = normalizeLyricCues(o.cues);
  201 | 
  202 |   // groupMap is optional; keep it only if it looks like the right shape
  203 |   const groupMap =
  204 |     o.groupMap && typeof o.groupMap === "object" && !Array.isArray(o.groupMap)
  205 |       ? (o.groupMap as LyricGroupMap)
  206 |       : undefined;
  207 | 
  208 |   const out: TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  209 |     ok: true,
  210 |     recordingId: o.recordingId.trim(),
  211 |     offsetMs: Math.floor(o.offsetMs),
  212 |     cues,
  213 |   };
  214 | 
  215 |   // Optional fields (keep them if present, but don’t over-validate here)
  216 |   if (typeof o.version === "string" && o.version.trim())
  217 |     out.version = o.version.trim();
  218 |   if (typeof o.geniusUrl === "string" || o.geniusUrl === null)
  219 |     out.geniusUrl = o.geniusUrl as string | null;
  220 |   if (groupMap) out.groupMap = groupMap;
  221 | 
  222 |   // Nullable meta fields (type-safe, no `any`)
  223 |   const nullableMetaKeys = [
  224 |     "trackTitle",
  225 |     "trackArtist",
  226 |     "trackCatalogueId",
  227 |     "albumTitle",
  228 |     "albumSlug",
  229 |     "albumCatalogueId",
  230 |   ] as const;
  231 | 
  232 |   type NullableMetaKey = (typeof nullableMetaKeys)[number];
  233 | 
  234 |   for (const k of nullableMetaKeys) {
  235 |     const v = o[k];
  236 |     if (typeof v === "string") {
  237 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = v.trim() || null;
  238 |     } else if (v === null) {
  239 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = null;
  240 |     }
  241 |   }
  242 | 
  243 |   return out;
  244 | }
